import react from '@vitejs/plugin-react';
import path from 'path';
import {defineConfig} from 'vite';

export default defineConfig(() => {
  return {
    plugins: [react(), tailwindcss()],
    resolve: {
      alias: {
        '@': path.resolve(__dirname, '.'),
      },
    },
    server: {
      // HMR is disabled in AI Studio via DISABLE_HMR env var.
      // Do not modifyâfile watching is disabled to prevent flickering during agent edits.
      hmr: process.env.DISABLE_HMR !== 'true',
      // Disable file watching when DISABLE_HMR is true to save CPU during agent edits.
      watch: process.env.DISABLE_HMR === 'true' ? null : {},
    },
  };
});

  name: string;
  nameEn: string;
  nameIt?: string;
  category: 'panini' | 'sandwich' | 'wrap' | 'sides' | 'drinks';
  price: number;
  weight: number; // in grams
  image: string;
  ingredients: string[]; // with grams e.g. "Schiacciata 180g"
  ingredientsEn?: string[];
  ingredientsIt?: string[];

  descriptionEn: string;
  descriptionIt?: string;
  tags: string[];
  tagsIt?: string[];

  allergensIt?: string[];

}

}

  color: string;
  icon?: string;
}

export type Language = 'ro' | 'en' | 'it';

=== FILE: src/utils/apiKeyManager.ts ===
// Utility to safely manage and access API Keys in the browser
// Handles missing keys, 401 Unauthorized, and dynamic runtime configuration

const STORAGE_KEY = 'sandwichef_api_key';

export interface ApiKeyStatus {
  hasKey: boolean;
  keySource: 'localStorage' | 'env' | 'none';
  maskedKey: string;
}

/**
 * Retrieves the currently active API Key safely
 */
export function getStoredApiKey(): string {
  try {
    const local = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
    if (local && local.trim().length > 0) {
      return local.trim();
    }
  } catch (e) {
    // Ignore localStorage block in restrictive iframes
  }

  // Fallback to environment variable if configured
  try {
    const envKey = (import.meta as any).env?.VITE_GEMINI_API_KEY || (import.meta as any).env?.VITE_API_KEY;
    if (typeof envKey === 'string' && envKey.trim().length > 0) {
      return envKey.trim();
    }
  } catch (e) {
    // Ignore
  }

  return '';
}

/**
 * Saves a user-provided API key to localStorage safely
 */
export function saveApiKey(key: string): boolean {
  try {
    const cleanKey = key.trim();
    if (cleanKey.length === 0) {
      localStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_KEY);
    } else {
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, cleanKey);
    }
    // Dispatch custom event for reactive UI updates
    window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('sandwichef_apikey_changed', { detail: { hasKey: cleanKey.length > 0 } }));
    return true;
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn('Could not save API key to localStorage:', e);
    return false;
  }
}

/**
 * Clears stored API key
 */
export function clearApiKey(): void {
  try {
    localStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_KEY);
    window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('sandwichef_apikey_changed', { detail: { hasKey: false } }));
  } catch (e) {
    // Ignore
  }
}

/**
 * Returns API key status and source
 */
export function getApiKeyStatus(): ApiKeyStatus {
  const key = getStoredApiKey();
  if (!key) {
    return {
      hasKey: false,
      keySource: 'none',
      maskedKey: '',
    };
  }

  let isFromLocal = false;
  try {
    isFromLocal = !!localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
  } catch (e) {
    isFromLocal = false;
  }

  const masked = key.length > 8 ? `${key.substring(0, 4)}...${key.substring(key.length - 4)}` : '••••••••';

  return {
    hasKey: true,
    keySource: isFromLocal ? 'localStorage' : 'env',
    maskedKey: masked,
  };
}

/**
 * Safe fetch wrapper that handles 401 Unauthorized without crashing the app
 */
export async function safeApiCall<T>(
  apiFunction: () => Promise<T>,
  fallbackValue: T,
  onError?: (error: any, is401: boolean) => void
): Promise<{ data: T; isError: boolean; is401: boolean; error?: any }> {
  try {
    const data = await apiFunction();
    return { data, isError: false, is401: false };
  } catch (err: any) {
    const errorMessage = err?.message || String(err);
    const status = err?.status || err?.response?.status;
    const is401 = status === 401 || errorMessage.includes('401') || errorMessage.toLowerCase().includes('unauthorized') || errorMessage.toLowerCase().includes('api key not valid');

    console.warn('[Sandwichef API Safe Guard] Caught API Error gracefully:', {
      is401,
      errorMessage,
    });

    if (onError) {
      onError(err, is401);
    }

    if (is401) {
      window.dispatchEvent(
        new CustomEvent('sandwichef_api_401', {
          detail: { error: errorMessage },
        })
      );
    }

    return {
      data: fallbackValue,
      isError: true,
      is401,
      error: err,
    };
  }
}

import React from 'react';

// Safe, high-availability fallback images
// All URLs are verified high-speed CDNs (Unsplash) or local assets in public/
export const FALLBACK_IMAGES = {
  // Local real logo or clean SVG
  logo: '/images/logo.png',
  logoSvg:
    'data:image/svg+xml;utf8,<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="100" height="100" viewBox="0 0 100 100"><circle cx="50" cy="50" r="50" fill="%23C44E36"/><text x="50" y="58" font-family="serif" font-size="34" font-weight="bold" fill="%23FFFFFF" text-anchor="middle">S</text></svg>',
  // Cover / hero banner
  cover: '/images/swc-cover.jpg',
  coverUnsplash:
    'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1509722747041-616f39b57569?auto=format&fit=crop&w=1200&q=80',
  // Artisan gourmet sandwich / panini
  sandwich:
    'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1528735602780-2552fd46c7af?auto=format&fit=crop&w=800&q=80',
  // Fresh Italian ingredients / builder
  builder:
    'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1550547660-d9450f859349?auto=format&fit=crop&w=800&q=80',
  // Catering platter
  catering:
    'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1555244162-803834f70033?auto=format&fit=crop&w=1000&q=80',
  // Drinks
  drink:
    'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1513558161293-cdaf765ed2fd?auto=format&fit=crop&w=800&q=80',
  // Dessert / sweets
  dessert:
    'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1579372786545-d24232daf58c?auto=format&fit=crop&w=800&q=80',
};

/**
 * Safe image onError handler
 * 1. Resets e.currentTarget.onerror = null immediately to break any infinite loop.
 * 2. Switches source to a safe fallback image.
 */
export function handleSafeImageError(
  e: React.SyntheticEvent<HTMLImageElement>,
  fallbackSrc: string = FALLBACK_IMAGES.sandwich
): void {
  const target = e.currentTarget;
  // CRITICAL: Prevent recursive calls & infinite re-render loops
  target.onerror = null;
  if (target.src !== fallbackSrc) {
    target.src = fallbackSrc;
  }
}

=== FILE: src/utils/status.ts ===
export interface StoreStatus {
  isOpen: boolean;
  statusBadgeRo: string;
  statusBadgeEn: string;
  statusBadgeIt: string;
  scheduleSnippetRo: string;
  scheduleSnippetEn: string;
  scheduleSnippetIt: string;
  statusTextRo: string;
  statusTextEn: string;
  statusTextIt: string;
  subtextRo: string;
  subtextEn: string;
  subtextIt: string;
}

export function getStoreStatus(): StoreStatus {
  try {
    const now = new Date();
    // Robust date & time extraction in Europe/Bucharest timezone
    const formatter = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US', {
      timeZone: 'Europe/Bucharest',
      hour12: false,
      weekday: 'short',
      hour: 'numeric',
      minute: 'numeric',
    });

    const parts = formatter.formatToParts(now);
    let weekdayStr = 'Mon';
    let hours = 10;
    let minutes = 0;

    for (const part of parts) {
      if (part.type === 'weekday') weekdayStr = part.value; // 'Mon', 'Tue', 'Wed', 'Thu', 'Fri', 'Sat', 'Sun'
      if (part.type === 'hour') {
        const val = parseInt(part.value, 10);
        hours = val === 24 ? 0 : val;
      }
      if (part.type === 'minute') minutes = parseInt(part.value, 10);
    }

    const currentMinutes = hours * 60 + minutes;
    const openMinutes = 9 * 60 + 30; // 09:30 (570 minutes)
    const closeMinutes = 17 * 60 + 30; // 17:30 (1050 minutes)

    const isMondayToThursday = ['Mon', 'Tue', 'Wed', 'Thu'].includes(weekdayStr);
    const isFriday = weekdayStr === 'Fri';
    const isWeekday = isMondayToThursday || isFriday;
    const isWithinHours = isWeekday && currentMinutes >= openMinutes && currentMinutes < closeMinutes;

    if (isWithinHours) {
      const remainingMinutes = closeMinutes - currentMinutes;
      const hoursLeft = Math.floor(remainingMinutes / 60);
      const minsLeft = remainingMinutes % 60;

      return {
        isOpen: true,
        statusBadgeRo: 'Deschis acum',
        statusBadgeEn: 'Open now',
        statusBadgeIt: 'Aperto ora',
        scheduleSnippetRo: '09:30 - 17:30',
        scheduleSnippetEn: '09:30 - 17:30',
        scheduleSnippetIt: '09:30 - 17:30',
        statusTextRo: 'Deschis acum în Craiova',
        statusTextEn: 'Open now in Craiova',
        statusTextIt: 'Aperto ora a Craiova',
        subtextRo: `Închidem la 17:30 (în ${hoursLeft > 0 ? `${hoursLeft}h ` : ''}${minsLeft} min)`,
        subtextEn: `Closes at 17:30 (in ${hoursLeft > 0 ? `${hoursLeft}h ` : ''}${minsLeft} min)`,
        subtextIt: `Chiude alle 17:30 (tra ${hoursLeft > 0 ? `${hoursLeft}h ` : ''}${minsLeft} min)`,
      };
    } else {
      let nextOpenRo = 'Luni la 09:30';
      let nextOpenEn = 'Monday at 09:30';
      let nextOpenIt = 'Lunedì alle 09:30';

      if (isWeekday && currentMinutes < openMinutes) {
        nextOpenRo = 'Azi la 09:30';
        nextOpenEn = 'Today at 09:30';
        nextOpenIt = 'Oggi alle 09:30';
      } else if (isMondayToThursday && currentMinutes >= closeMinutes) {
        nextOpenRo = 'Mâine la 09:30';
        nextOpenEn = 'Tomorrow at 09:30';
        nextOpenIt = 'Domani alle 09:30';
      } else {
        // Friday after 17:30, Saturday, Sunday
        nextOpenRo = 'Luni la 09:30';
        nextOpenEn = 'Monday at 09:30';
        nextOpenIt = 'Lunedì alle 09:30';
      }

      return {
        isOpen: false,
        statusBadgeRo: 'Momentan închis',
        statusBadgeEn: 'Currently closed',
        statusBadgeIt: 'Attualmente chiuso',
        scheduleSnippetRo: `Deschidem ${nextOpenRo.toLowerCase()}`,
        scheduleSnippetEn: `Opens ${nextOpenEn.toLowerCase()}`,
        scheduleSnippetIt: `Apre ${nextOpenIt.toLowerCase()}`,
        statusTextRo: 'Momentan închis',
        statusTextEn: 'Currently closed',
        statusTextIt: 'Attualmente chiuso',
        subtextRo: `Deschidem ${nextOpenRo} (Str. Amaradia 29)`,
        subtextEn: `Opens ${nextOpenEn} (Amaradia St. 29)`,
        subtextIt: `Apre ${nextOpenIt} (Via Amaradia 29)`,
      };
    }
  } catch (e) {
    return {
      isOpen: false,
      statusBadgeRo: 'Program L-V',
      statusBadgeEn: 'Mon-Fri schedule',
      statusBadgeIt: 'Orario Lun-Ven',
      scheduleSnippetRo: '09:30 - 17:30',
      scheduleSnippetEn: '09:30 - 17:30',
      scheduleSnippetIt: '09:30 - 17:30',
      statusTextRo: 'Program: Luni - Vineri 09:30 - 17:30',
      statusTextEn: 'Hours: Mon - Fri 09:30 - 17:30',
      statusTextIt: 'Orario: Lun - Ven 09:30 - 17:30',
      subtextRo: 'Str. Amaradia, Nr. 29, Craiova',
      subtextEn: '29 Amaradia St., Craiova',
      subtextIt: 'Via Amaradia, N. 29, Craiova',
    };
  }
}

=== FILE: src/components/ApiKeyModal.tsx ===
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { KeyRound, X, Check, Trash2, Eye, EyeOff, Sparkles, ShieldCheck } from 'lucide-react';
import { getStoredApiKey, saveApiKey, clearApiKey, getApiKeyStatus } from '../utils/apiKeyManager';
import { Language } from '../types';

interface ApiKeyModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  lang: Language;
}

export const ApiKeyModal: React.FC<ApiKeyModalProps> = ({ isOpen, onClose, lang }) => {
  const [keyInput, setKeyInput] = useState(getStoredApiKey());
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
  const [statusMessage, setStatusMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const status = getApiKeyStatus();

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const handleSave = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const clean = keyInput.trim();
    saveApiKey(clean);
    setStatusMessage(
      lang === 'ro'
        ? 'Cheia API a fost salvată cu succes!'
        : lang === 'it'
        ? 'Chiave API salvata con successo!'
        : 'API Key successfully saved!'
    );
    setTimeout(() => {
      setStatusMessage(null);
      onClose();
    }, 1200);
  };

  const handleClear = () => {
    clearApiKey();
    setKeyInput('');
    setStatusMessage(
      lang === 'ro'
        ? 'Cheia a fost ștearsă.'
        : lang === 'it'
        ? 'Chiave rimossa.'
        : 'API Key cleared.'
    );
    setTimeout(() => setStatusMessage(null), 1500);
  };

  return (
    <div
      id="api-key-modal-backdrop"
      onClick={onClose}
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4 animate-in fade-in duration-200"
    >
      <div
        id="api-key-modal-content"
        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
        className="w-full max-w-md bg-[#FAF7F2] rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-7 border border-[#E4D9CA] shadow-2xl relative overflow-hidden"
      >
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="flex items-start justify-between pb-4 border-b border-[#241813]/10 mb-5">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-2xl bg-[#C44E36]/15 text-[#C44E36] flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
              <KeyRound className="w-5 h-5" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h3 className="font-serif text-lg font-bold text-[#241813]">
                {lang === 'ro' ? 'Configurare Cheie API' : lang === 'it' ? 'Configurazione Chiave API' : 'API Key Settings'}
              </h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-[#6B5E55]">
                {lang === 'ro'
                  ? 'Gestionare conexiune servicii AI & Cloud'
                  : lang === 'it'
                  ? 'Gestione connessione servizi AI & Cloud'
                  : 'Manage AI & Cloud service connection'}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={onClose}
            className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-[#241813]/5 hover:bg-[#241813]/10 flex items-center justify-center text-[#241813] transition-colors cursor-pointer"
          >
            <X className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Current status pill */}
        <div className="mb-4 p-3 rounded-2xl bg-white border border-[#E4D9CA] flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span
              className={`w-2 h-2 rounded-full ${
                status.hasKey ? 'bg-emerald-500' : 'bg-amber-500'
              }`}
            />
            <span className="font-semibold text-[#241813]">
              {status.hasKey
                ? lang === 'ro'
                  ? `Cheie activă (${status.keySource})`
                  : lang === 'it'
                  ? `Chiave attiva (${status.keySource})`
                  : `Active key (${status.keySource})`
                : lang === 'ro'
                ? 'Nicio cheie configurată (mod local)'
                : lang === 'it'
                ? 'Nessuna chiave (modalità locale)'
                : 'No key configured (local mode)'}
            </span>
          </div>
          {status.hasKey && (
            <span className="font-mono text-[11px] text-[#6B5E55] bg-[#FAF7F2] px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md border border-[#E4D9CA]">
              {status.maskedKey}
            </span>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Form */}
        <form onSubmit={handleSave} className="space-y-4">
          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-[#241813] uppercase tracking-wider mb-1.5">
              {lang === 'ro' ? 'Cheie API personalizată' : lang === 'it' ? 'Chiave API personalizzata' : 'Custom API Key'}
            </label>
            <div className="relative">
              <input
                type={showPassword ? 'text' : 'password'}
                value={keyInput}
                onChange={(e) => setKeyInput(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="AIzaSy..."
                className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 pr-11 rounded-xl bg-white border border-[#E4D9CA] text-[#241813] placeholder-[#8C7E74]/60 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-[#C44E36] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#C44E36] transition-all font-mono"
              />
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setShowPassword(!showPassword)}
                className="absolute right-2.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 p-1 text-[#8C7E74] hover:text-[#241813] transition-colors"
              >
                {showPassword ? <EyeOff className="w-4 h-4" /> : <Eye className="w-4 h-4" />}
              </button>
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] text-[#8C7E74] mt-2">
              <ShieldCheck className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-600 shrink-0" />
              <span>
                {lang === 'ro'
                  ? 'Stocare securizată exclusiv în browserul tău local.'
                  : lang === 'it'
                  ? 'Archiviazione sicura esclusivamente nel tuo browser.'
                  : 'Securely saved in local browser storage only.'}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>

          {statusMessage && (
            <div className="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 text-xs font-semibold text-emerald-800 flex items-center gap-2">
              <Check className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-600 shrink-0" />
              <span>{statusMessage}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 pt-2">
            <button
              type="submit"
              className="flex-1 py-2.5 px-4 rounded-xl bg-[#C44E36] hover:bg-[#A83D28] text-white font-bold text-xs tracking-wide shadow-xs active:scale-98 transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 cursor-pointer"
            >
              <Check className="w-4 h-4" />
              <span>{lang === 'ro' ? 'Salvează Cheia' : lang === 'it' ? 'Salva Chiave' : 'Save Key'}</span>
            </button>

            {status.hasKey && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleClear}
                className="py-2.5 px-3 rounded-xl bg-[#241813]/5 hover:bg-rose-50 text-[#8C7E74] hover:text-rose-600 border border-[#E4D9CA] font-semibold text-xs transition-all cursor-pointer"
                title="Șterge cheia"
              >
                <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4" />
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        </form>

        {/* Note */}
        <p className="text-[11px] text-[#6B5E55] text-center mt-4">
          {lang === 'ro'
            ? 'Notă: Meniul complet și comenzile WhatsApp funcționează perfect și fără cheie API.'
            : lang === 'it'
            ? 'Nota: Il menu completo e gli ordini WhatsApp funzionano perfettamente anche senza chiave API.'
            : 'Note: Full menu browsing and WhatsApp orders work perfectly without an API key.'}
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

=== END FILE ===

=== FILE: src/components/ErrorBoundary.tsx ===
import React, { ErrorInfo, ReactNode } from 'react';
import { KeyRound, AlertTriangle, RefreshCw, ArrowRight, ShieldAlert, CheckCircle2, Eye, EyeOff } from 'lucide-react';
import { getStoredApiKey, saveApiKey, clearApiKey } from '../utils/apiKeyManager';

export interface ErrorBoundaryProps {
  children: ReactNode;
  fallback?: ReactNode;
}

export interface ErrorBoundaryState {
  hasError: boolean;
  is401: boolean;
  errorMessage: string;
  userApiKey: string;
  showKeyInput: boolean;
  saveSuccess: boolean;
  safeModeActive: boolean;
  showPassword: boolean;
}

export class ErrorBoundary extends React.Component<ErrorBoundaryProps, ErrorBoundaryState> {
  constructor(props: ErrorBoundaryProps) {
    super(props);
    this.state = {
      hasError: false,
      is401: false,
      errorMessage: '',
      userApiKey: getStoredApiKey(),
      showKeyInput: false,
      saveSuccess: false,
      safeModeActive: false,
      showPassword: false,
    };
  }

  private unhandledRejectionListener = (event: PromiseRejectionEvent) => {
    const reason = event.reason;
    const msg = typeof reason === 'string' ? reason : reason?.message || JSON.stringify(reason);
    const is401 =
      msg?.includes('401') ||
      msg?.toLowerCase().includes('unauthorized') ||
      msg?.toLowerCase().includes('api key not valid') ||
      msg?.toLowerCase().includes('api_key_invalid');

    if (is401) {
      console.warn('[ErrorBoundary] Caught 401 Unauthorized in unhandled promise:', msg);
      this.setState({
        hasError: true,
        is401: true,
        errorMessage: msg,
        showKeyInput: true,
      });
      // Prevent browser default white screen
      event.preventDefault();
    }
  };

  private custom401Listener = (event: Event) => {
    const customEvent = event as CustomEvent;
    this.setState({
      hasError: true,
      is401: true,
      errorMessage: customEvent.detail?.error || '401 Unauthorized - API Key invalid sau expirat',
      showKeyInput: true,
    });
  };

  public static getDerivedStateFromError(error: Error): Partial<ErrorBoundaryState> {
    const msg = error.message || '';
    const is401 =
      msg.includes('401') ||
      msg.toLowerCase().includes('unauthorized') ||
      msg.toLowerCase().includes('api key') ||
      msg.toLowerCase().includes('apikey');

    return {
      hasError: true,
      is401,
      errorMessage: msg,
      showKeyInput: is401 || msg.includes('key'),
    };
  }

  public override componentDidMount() {
    window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection', this.unhandledRejectionListener);
    window.addEventListener('sandwichef_api_401', this.custom401Listener);
  }

  public override componentWillUnmount() {
    window.removeEventListener('unhandledrejection', this.unhandledRejectionListener);
    window.removeEventListener('sandwichef_api_401', this.custom401Listener);
  }

  public override componentDidCatch(error: Error, errorInfo: ErrorInfo) {
    console.error('[Sandwichef ErrorBoundary caught an exception]:', error, errorInfo);
  }

  private handleSaveKey = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const success = saveApiKey(this.state.userApiKey);
    if (success) {
      this.setState({ saveSuccess: true });
      setTimeout(() => {
        // Attempt clean recovery
        this.setState({
          hasError: false,
          is401: false,
          saveSuccess: false,
        });
        window.location.reload();
      }, 800);
    }
  };

  private handleBypassToSafeMode = () => {
    // Allows loading the standard visual catalogue, ordering, and artisan builder without crashing
    this.setState({
      hasError: false,
      is401: false,
      safeModeActive: true,
    });
  };

  private handleReset = () => {
    clearApiKey();
    this.setState({
      userApiKey: '',
      hasError: false,
      is401: false,
    });
    window.location.reload();
  };

  public override render() {
    if (this.state.hasError && !this.state.safeModeActive) {
      return (
        <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#1F1714] text-[#FAF7F2] flex flex-col justify-center items-center px-4 py-12 selection:bg-[#C44E36] selection:text-white">
          {/* Decorative Warm Glow */}
          <div className="absolute top-1/4 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 w-96 h-96 bg-[#C44E36]/15 rounded-full blur-3xl pointer-events-none" />

          <div className="relative z-10 w-full max-w-lg bg-[#2A1F1B] border border-[#E4D9CA]/15 rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 shadow-2xl backdrop-blur-md">
            {/* Header Icon */}
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-5">
              <div
                className={`w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 ${
                  this.state.is401 ? 'bg-[#F0BE2C]/20 text-[#F0BE2C] border border-[#F0BE2C]/30' : 'bg-[#C44E36]/20 text-[#E0654B] border border-[#C44E36]/30'
                }`}
              >
                {this.state.is401 ? <KeyRound className="w-6 h-6" /> : <AlertTriangle className="w-6 h-6" />}
              </div>
              <div>
                <span className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-[#F0BE2C]">
                  {this.state.is401 ? 'Autentificare API Necesară (401)' : 'Recuperare Stare Aplicație'}
                </span>
                <h2 className="font-serif text-xl sm:text-2xl font-bold text-white leading-tight">
                  {this.state.is401 ? 'Configurare Cheie API' : 'A apărut o întrerupere temporară'}
                </h2>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Explanatory Note */}
            <p className="text-sm text-[#E4D9CA]/80 leading-relaxed mb-6">
              {this.state.is401
                ? 'Apelul inițial a primit un răspuns 401 (Unauthorized) sau cheia API este absentă / invalidă. Poți introduce propria cheie sau poți continua direct în modul Meniu & Comandă WhatsApp.'
                : 'Aplicația a interceptat o eroare la randare și a prevenit afișarea unui ecran alb. Poți continua navigarea în modul protejat sau poți reîncărca pagina.'}
            </p>

            {/* Error Detail Pill (if exists) */}
            {this.state.errorMessage && (
              <div className="mb-6 p-3 rounded-xl bg-black/40 border border-white/10 text-xs font-mono text-stone-300 break-all flex items-start gap-2 max-h-24 overflow-y-auto">
                <ShieldAlert className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                <span className="flex-1">{this.state.errorMessage}</span>
              </div>
            )}

            {/* API Key Form */}
            <form onSubmit={this.handleSaveKey} className="space-y-4 mb-6">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-[#E4D9CA] uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">
                  Cheie API (Gemini / AI Studio / Custom)
                </label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <input
                    type={this.state.showPassword ? 'text' : 'password'}
                    value={this.state.userApiKey}
                    onChange={(e) => this.setState({ userApiKey: e.target.value })}
                    placeholder="Introdu cheia ta (AIzaSy...)"
                    className="w-full px-4 py-3 pr-12 rounded-xl bg-[#1F1714] border border-[#E4D9CA]/25 text-white placeholder-stone-500 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-[#F0BE2C] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#F0BE2C] transition-all"
                  />
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => this.setState({ showPassword: !this.state.showPassword })}
                    className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 p-1 text-stone-400 hover:text-white transition-colors"
                  >
                    {this.state.showPassword ? <EyeOff className="w-4 h-4" /> : <Eye className="w-4 h-4" />}
                  </button>
                </div>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-[#A89F91] mt-1.5">
                  Cheia este salvată exclusiv local în browserul tău (<code className="text-amber-300">localStorage</code>) și nu este transmisă către terți.
                </p>
              </div>

              <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-2.5 pt-1">
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  className="flex-1 inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-5 py-3 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-[#C44E36] to-[#E0654B] hover:from-[#A83D28] hover:to-[#C44E36] text-white font-bold text-sm shadow-md active:scale-98 transition-all cursor-pointer"
                >
                  {this.state.saveSuccess ? (
                    <>
                      <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-300" />
                      <span>Salvat! Se repornește...</span>
                    </>
                  ) : (
                    <>
                      <KeyRound className="w-4 h-4" />
                      <span>Salvează & Reîncarcă</span>
                    </>
                  )}
                </button>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={this.handleBypassToSafeMode}
                  className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-4 py-3 rounded-xl bg-white/10 hover:bg-white/15 border border-white/15 text-white font-semibold text-sm transition-all active:scale-98 cursor-pointer"
                >
                  <span>Meniu & Comandă</span>
                  <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>

            {/* Footer Secondary Actions */}
            <div className="pt-4 border-t border-white/10 flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-[#A89F91]">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={this.handleReset}
                className="hover:text-white flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
              >
                <RefreshCw className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                <span>Resetează cheia stocată</span>
              </button>

              <span className="text-[11px] text-stone-500">Sandwichef Craiova Guard</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      );
    }

    return this.props.children;
  }
}

import React from 'react';
import { Language } from '../types';

export const FlagRO: React.FC<{ className?: string }> = ({ className = 'w-4 h-3' }) => (

    className={`${className} inline-block shrink-0 rounded-2xs overflow-hidden shadow-2xs border border-black/10`}
    viewBox="0 0 3 2"
    aria-hidden="true"
  >
    <rect width="1" height="2" fill="#002B7F" />

    viewBox="0 0 60 30"
    aria-hidden="true"

export const FlagIT: React.FC<{ className?: string }> = ({ className = 'w-4 h-3' }) => (
  <svg
    className={`${className} inline-block shrink-0 rounded-2xs overflow-hidden shadow-2xs border border-black/10`}

    <rect x="2" width="1" height="2" fill="#CE2B37" />

interface LanguageSwitcherProps {
  currentLang: Language;
  onSelectLang: (lang: Language) => void;
  size?: 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg';
  className?: string;
}

export const LanguageSwitcher: React.FC<LanguageSwitcherProps> = ({
  currentLang,
  onSelectLang,
  size = 'sm',
  className = '',
}) => {
  const languages: { id: Language; label: string; name: string; Flag: React.FC<{ className?: string }> }[] = [
    { id: 'ro', label: 'RO', name: 'Română', Flag: FlagRO },
    { id: 'en', label: 'EN', name: 'English', Flag: FlagEN },
    { id: 'it', label: 'IT', name: 'Italiano', Flag: FlagIT },
  ];

  return (
    <div
      id="language-switcher-group"
      className={`inline-flex items-center bg-[#F0EAE1] rounded-full p-0.5 border border-[#E4D9CA] ${className}`}
      role="group"
      aria-label="Alege limba / Select language / Scegli la lingua"
    >
      {languages.map(({ id, label, name, Flag }) => {
        const isSelected = currentLang === id;
        return (
          <button
            key={id}
            id={`lang-btn-${id}`}
            type="button"
            onClick={() => onSelectLang(id)}
            title={name}
            aria-pressed={isSelected}
            className={`flex items-center gap-1 sm:gap-1.5 transition-all duration-200 cursor-pointer font-bold rounded-full shrink-0 ${
              size === 'sm'
                ? 'px-1.5 sm:px-2 py-0.5 sm:py-1 text-[10px] sm:text-[11px]'
                : size === 'md'
                ? 'px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs'
                : 'px-3.5 py-2 text-sm'
            } ${
              isSelected
                ? 'bg-[#C44E36] text-white shadow-xs font-extrabold'
                : 'text-[#5C4D44] hover:text-[#241813] hover:bg-white/40'
            }`}
          >
            <Flag className={size === 'sm' ? 'w-3.5 h-2.5 shrink-0' : 'w-4 h-3 shrink-0'} />
            <span className="leading-none">{label}</span>
          </button>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
};

                </p>
                <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-[#F0BE2C] truncate">

                </p>

                  {lang === 'ro' ? 'Apasă pentru apel direct' : lang === 'it' ? 'Tocca per chiamare subito' : 'Tap for instant call'}
                </p>
              </div>
            </a>

      </div>
    </section>
  );
};

=== FILE: src/components/Navbar.tsx ===
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Phone, Menu, X, Clock, MapPin, Sparkles, MessageCircle, ExternalLink, Globe } from 'lucide-react';
import { Language } from '../types';
import { getStoreStatus } from '../utils/status';
import { LanguageSwitcher } from './LanguageFlags';
import { handleSafeImageError, FALLBACK_IMAGES } from '../utils/imageFallback';

interface NavbarProps {
  cartCount: number;
  onOpenCart: () => void;
  onNavigateTo: (id: string) => void;
  lang: Language;
  onToggleLang?: () => void;
  setLang?: (lang: Language) => void;
  activeTab?: string;
  setActiveTab?: (tab: string) => void;
  openCart?: () => void;
}

export const Navbar: React.FC<NavbarProps> = ({
  cartCount,
  onOpenCart,
  onNavigateTo,
  lang,
  onToggleLang,
  setLang,
  activeTab = 'home',
  setActiveTab,
  openCart,
}) => {
  const [scrolled, setScrolled] = useState(false);
  const [mobileMenuOpen, setMobileMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const [storeStatus, setStoreStatus] = useState(getStoreStatus());

  useEffect(() => {
    const update = () => setStoreStatus(getStoreStatus());
    update();
    const interval = setInterval(update, 30000);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, []);

  // Prevent background scroll when mobile menu is open
  useEffect(() => {
    if (mobileMenuOpen) {
      document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
    } else {
      document.body.style.overflow = '';
    }
    return () => {
      document.body.style.overflow = '';
    };
  }, [mobileMenuOpen]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleScroll = () => {
      setScrolled(window.scrollY > 20);
    };
    window.addEventListener('scroll', handleScroll);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('scroll', handleScroll);
  }, []);

  const navLinks = [
    { id: 'home', labelRo: 'Acasă', labelEn: 'Home', labelIt: 'Inizio' },
    { id: 'menu', labelRo: 'Meniu complet', labelEn: 'Full menu', labelIt: 'Menu completo' },
    { id: 'builder', labelRo: 'Chef Lab ✨', labelEn: 'Chef Lab ✨', labelIt: 'Chef Lab ✨' },
    { id: 'catering', labelRo: 'Catering evenimente', labelEn: 'Catering', labelIt: 'Catering eventi' },
    { id: 'location', labelRo: 'Locație & contact', labelEn: 'Location & contact', labelIt: 'Posizione & contatti' },
  ];

  const handleNavClick = (id: string) => {
    if (setActiveTab) setActiveTab(id);
    onNavigateTo(id);
    setMobileMenuOpen(false);
  };

  const handleSetLang = (newLang: Language) => {
    if (setLang) {
      setLang(newLang);
    } else if (onToggleLang) {
      onToggleLang();
    }
  };

  const statusText =
    lang === 'ro'
      ? storeStatus.statusTextRo
      : lang === 'it'
      ? storeStatus.statusTextIt
      : storeStatus.statusTextEn;

  return (
    <>
      <header
        id="main-navbar"
        className={`fixed top-0 left-0 right-0 z-50 transition-all duration-300 ${
          scrolled
            ? 'bg-[#FAF7F2]/95 backdrop-blur-md shadow-xs py-2 sm:py-2.5 border-b border-[#241813]/10'
            : 'bg-[#FAF7F2]/90 backdrop-blur-sm py-2.5 sm:py-3.5 border-b border-[#241813]/5'
        }`}
      >
        <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-3 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            {/* Brand Logo & Name */}
            <button
              id="brand-logo-btn"
              onClick={() => handleNavClick('home')}
              className="flex items-center gap-2.5 sm:gap-3 group text-left cursor-pointer focus:outline-none min-h-[44px]"
            >
              <div className="relative w-9 h-9 sm:w-11 sm:h-11 rounded-full overflow-hidden border-2 border-[#C44E36] shadow-xs group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform shrink-0">
                <img
                  src="/images/logo.png"
                  alt="Sandwichef Logo"
                  className="w-full h-full object-cover"
                  onError={(e) => {
                    handleSafeImageError(e, FALLBACK_IMAGES.logoSvg);
                  }}
                />
              </div>
              <div className="flex flex-col">
                <span className="font-serif text-xl sm:text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-[#241813] group-hover:text-[#C44E36] transition-colors leading-tight">
                  Sandwichef
                </span>
                <span className="font-bold text-[#C44E36] tracking-wider text-[10px] leading-none">
                  CRAIOVA
                </span>
              </div>
            </button>

            {/* Desktop Navigation Links */}
            <nav className="hidden lg:flex items-center gap-1 bg-[#F0EAE1] px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full border border-[#E4D9CA]">
              {navLinks.map((link) => {
                const isActive = activeTab === link.id;
                const label = lang === 'ro' ? link.labelRo : lang === 'it' ? link.labelIt : link.labelEn;
                return (
                  <button
                    key={link.id}
                    id={`nav-link-${link.id}`}
                    onClick={() => handleNavClick(link.id)}
                    className={`px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold rounded-full transition-all duration-200 cursor-pointer ${
                      isActive
                        ? 'bg-[#C44E36] text-white shadow-xs'
                        : 'text-[#4A3B32] hover:text-[#C44E36] hover:bg-white/60'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {label}
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </nav>

            {/* Right Actions */}

                title="Apelează Sandwichef Craiova"
              >

                <span>0735 468 253</span>
              </a>

              {/* Language Switcher with Flags for RO, EN, IT */}
              <LanguageSwitcher
                currentLang={lang}
                onSelectLang={handleSetLang}
                size="sm"
              />

              {/* Mobile Menu Hamburger Button */}
              <button
                id="mobile-menu-toggle"
                onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(!mobileMenuOpen)}
                className="lg:hidden p-2 rounded-xl text-[#241813] hover:bg-[#F0EAE1] active:bg-[#E4D9CA] transition-colors focus:outline-none cursor-pointer min-h-[40px] min-w-[40px] flex items-center justify-center"
                aria-label="Meniu de navigare"
              >
                {mobileMenuOpen ? <X className="w-6 h-6 text-[#C44E36]" /> : <Menu className="w-6 h-6" />}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>

          onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(false)}
        >
          <div
            id="mobile-menu-drawer-content"

                rel="noreferrer"
