=== FILE: src/components/SandwichLab.tsx ===
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import confetti from 'canvas-confetti';
import { Sparkles, Layers, Check, RotateCcw, ShoppingBag, Eye, EyeOff } from 'lucide-react';
import { BuilderOption, Language, MenuItem } from '../types';
import { BUILDER_OPTIONS } from '../data/menuData';

interface SandwichLabProps {
  onAddCustomSandwich: (
    item: MenuItem,
    details: {
      bread: string;
      proteins: string[];
      cheeses: string[];
      veggies: string[];
      sauces: string[];
      totalWeight: number;
    }
  ) => void;
  lang: Language;
}

export const SandwichLab: React.FC<SandwichLabProps> = ({
  onAddCustomSandwich,
  lang,
}) => {
  // Selections
  const [selectedBread, setSelectedBread] = useState<string>('b-schiacciata');
  const [selectedProteins, setSelectedProteins] = useState<string[]>(['p-mortadella']);
  const [selectedCheeses, setSelectedCheeses] = useState<string[]>(['c-gorgonzola']);
  const [selectedVeggies, setSelectedVeggies] = useState<string[]>(['v-sundried', 'v-rucola']);
  const [selectedSauces, setSelectedSauces] = useState<string[]>(['s-pesto']);
  const [sandwichName, setSandwichName] = useState<string>('Panini Il Magnifico Craiova');
  const [showMobilePreview, setShowMobilePreview] = useState<boolean>(false);

  const breads = BUILDER_OPTIONS.filter((o) => o.category === 'bread');
  const proteins = BUILDER_OPTIONS.filter((o) => o.category === 'protein');
  const cheeses = BUILDER_OPTIONS.filter((o) => o.category === 'cheese');
  const veggies = BUILDER_OPTIONS.filter((o) => o.category === 'veggies');
  const sauces = BUILDER_OPTIONS.filter((o) => o.category === 'sauce');

  // Toggle helpers
  const toggleArrayItem = (
    list: string[],
    setList: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<string[]>>,
    id: string
  ) => {
    if (list.includes(id)) {
      setList(list.filter((x) => x !== id));
    } else {
      setList([...list, id]);
    }
  };

  // Calculations
  const allSelectedIds = [
    selectedBread,
    ...selectedProteins,
    ...selectedCheeses,
    ...selectedVeggies,
    ...selectedSauces,
  ];

  const selectedOptions = BUILDER_OPTIONS.filter((o) => allSelectedIds.includes(o.id));
  const totalGrams = selectedOptions.reduce((sum, opt) => sum + opt.weight, 0);
  const totalPrice = selectedOptions.reduce((sum, opt) => sum + opt.price, 0);

  const handleReset = () => {
    setSelectedBread('b-schiacciata');
    setSelectedProteins(['p-mortadella']);
    setSelectedCheeses(['c-gorgonzola']);
    setSelectedVeggies(['v-sundried', 'v-rucola']);
    setSelectedSauces(['s-pesto']);
    setSandwichName('Panini Il Magnifico Craiova');
  };

  const handleCreateAndAddToCart = () => {
    try {
      confetti({
        particleCount: 80,
        spread: 70,
        origin: { y: 0.7 },
        colors: ['#C44E36', '#F0BE2C', '#241813'],
      });
    } catch (e) {
      // non-blocking
    }

    const breadObj = BUILDER_OPTIONS.find((b) => b.id === selectedBread);
    const breadName = breadObj
      ? lang === 'ro'
        ? breadObj.name
        : lang === 'it'
        ? (breadObj.nameIt || breadObj.name)
        : breadObj.nameEn
      : 'Pâine Artizanală';

    const proteinNames = selectedProteins
      .map((id) => BUILDER_OPTIONS.find((o) => o.id === id))
      .filter(Boolean)
      .map((o) => (lang === 'ro' ? o!.name : lang === 'it' ? (o!.nameIt || o!.name) : o!.nameEn));

    const cheeseNames = selectedCheeses
      .map((id) => BUILDER_OPTIONS.find((o) => o.id === id))
      .filter(Boolean)
      .map((o) => (lang === 'ro' ? o!.name : lang === 'it' ? (o!.nameIt || o!.name) : o!.nameEn));

    const veggieNames = selectedVeggies
      .map((id) => BUILDER_OPTIONS.find((o) => o.id === id))
      .filter(Boolean)
      .map((o) => (lang === 'ro' ? o!.name : lang === 'it' ? (o!.nameIt || o!.name) : o!.nameEn));

    const sauceNames = selectedSauces
      .map((id) => BUILDER_OPTIONS.find((o) => o.id === id))
      .filter(Boolean)
      .map((o) => (lang === 'ro' ? o!.name : lang === 'it' ? (o!.nameIt || o!.name) : o!.nameEn));

    const customItem: MenuItem = {
      id: `custom-${Date.now()}`,
      number: 99,
      name:
        sandwichName ||
        (lang === 'ro' ? 'Sandwich Personalizat Chef' : lang === 'it' ? 'Panino Personalizzato Chef' : 'Custom Chef Sandwich'),
      nameEn: sandwichName || 'Custom Chef Sandwich',
      nameIt: sandwichName || 'Panino Personalizzato Chef',
      category: 'panini',
      price: totalPrice,
      weight: totalGrams,
      image: '/images/swc-panini-mama-mia.jpeg',
      ingredients: [breadName, ...proteinNames, ...cheeseNames, ...veggieNames, ...sauceNames],
      description: `Creație unică: ${breadName}, cu ${proteinNames.join(', ')} și sosuri gourmet.`,
      descriptionEn: `Custom creation with ${breadName}, ${proteinNames.join(', ')} and sauces.`,
      descriptionIt: `Creazione unica: ${breadName}, con ${proteinNames.join(', ')} e salse gourmet.`,
      tags: ['Creație Proprie', 'Chef Lab', `${totalGrams}g`],
    };

    onAddCustomSandwich(customItem, {
      bread: breadName,
      proteins: proteinNames,
      cheeses: cheeseNames,
      veggies: veggieNames,
      sauces: sauceNames,
      totalWeight: totalGrams,
    });
  };

  return (
    <section id="builder" className="py-12 sm:py-20 bg-[#F5EFE6] border-y border-[#241813]/10 relative">
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-3 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
        {/* Section Header */}
        <div className="text-center max-w-2xl mx-auto mb-6 sm:mb-12">
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-[11px] sm:text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-[#C44E36] bg-[#C44E36]/10 mb-2 sm:mb-3">
            <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#F0BE2C]" />
            {lang === 'ro'
              ? 'Laboratorul Sandwichef'
              : lang === 'it'
              ? 'Laboratorio Sandwichef'
              : 'Sandwichef Creative Lab'}
          </span>
          <h2 className="font-serif text-2xl sm:text-4xl md:text-5xl font-bold text-[#241813] tracking-tight mb-2 sm:mb-3">
            {lang === 'ro'
              ? 'Fii tu chef-ul! Creează-ți sandwich-ul'
              : lang === 'it'
              ? 'Diventa tu lo chef! Crea il tuo panino'
              : 'Build your own sandwich'}
          </h2>
          <p className="text-xs sm:text-base text-[#6B5E55] leading-relaxed px-2">
            {lang === 'ro'
              ? 'Alege pâinea toscană, carnea rumenită, brânzeturile fine și sosurile. Calculăm live gramajul și prețul.'
              : lang === 'it'
              ? 'Scegli la schiacciata toscana, salumi saporiti, formaggi pregiati e salse fatte in casa. Calcolo in tempo reale di peso e prezzo.'
              : 'Pick your artisan bread, proteins, cheeses, and dressings. Real-time weight and price.'}
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* MOBILE STICKY FLOATING ACTION BAR FOR PHONES */}
        <div className="lg:hidden sticky top-16 sm:top-20 z-30 bg-white/95 backdrop-blur-md rounded-2xl p-3 mb-6 border border-[#E7DDCE] shadow-md flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="text-left">
              <span className="text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-[#8C7E74] block">
                {lang === 'ro' ? 'Total live' : lang === 'it' ? 'Totale live' : 'Live total'}
              </span>
              <span className="font-bold text-sm sm:text-base text-[#C44E36]">
                {totalPrice} RON
              </span>
              <span className="text-xs text-[#8C7E74] ml-1">({totalGrams}g)</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <button
              onClick={() => setShowMobilePreview(!showMobilePreview)}
              className="px-2.5 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold bg-[#FAF7F2] border border-[#E7DDCE] text-[#4A3B32] flex items-center gap-1 min-h-[40px]"
              aria-label="Arată previzualizare sandwich"
            >
              {showMobilePreview ? <EyeOff className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> : <Eye className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />}
              <span>
                {showMobilePreview
                  ? (lang === 'ro' ? 'Ascunde' : lang === 'it' ? 'Nascondi' : 'Hide')
                  : (lang === 'ro' ? 'Straturi' : lang === 'it' ? 'Strati' : 'Layers')}
              </span>
            </button>

            <button
              id="builder-mobile-quick-add-btn"
              onClick={handleCreateAndAddToCart}
              className="px-4 py-2 bg-[#C44E36] hover:bg-[#A63B25] active:scale-95 text-white font-bold rounded-xl text-xs flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-xs min-h-[40px]"
            >
              <ShoppingBag className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>{lang === 'ro' ? 'Adaugă' : lang === 'it' ? 'Aggiungi' : 'Add'}</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Collapsible Mobile Sandwich Stacker Preview */}
        {showMobilePreview && (
          <div className="lg:hidden bg-white p-4 rounded-2xl border border-[#E7DDCE] shadow-md mb-6 animate-in slide-in-from-top-3 duration-200">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
              <span className="text-xs font-bold text-[#241813] flex items-center gap-1">
                <Layers className="w-4 h-4 text-[#C44E36]" />
                <span>
                  {lang === 'ro'
                    ? `Previzualizare Sandwichef (${totalGrams}g)`
                    : lang === 'it'
                    ? `Anteprima Panino (${totalGrams}g)`
                    : `Sandwich Preview (${totalGrams}g)`}
                </span>
              </span>
              <button
                onClick={handleReset}
                className="text-[11px] font-bold text-[#8C7E74] hover:text-[#C44E36]"
              >
                {lang === 'ro' ? 'Resetează' : lang === 'it' ? 'Reimposta' : 'Reset'}
              </button>
            </div>

            {/* Visual Mini Stacker */}
            <div className="w-full max-w-[240px] mx-auto flex flex-col gap-1 items-center py-2">
              <div className="w-full h-6 rounded-t-xl bg-[#D99B52] border border-[#B37B39] shadow-2xs flex items-center justify-center text-[10px] font-bold text-[#4B2F10]">
                🍞 {BUILDER_OPTIONS.find((b) => b.id === selectedBread)?.name.split(' ')[0] || 'Pâine'} ({lang === 'ro' ? 'Capac' : lang === 'it' ? 'Sopra' : 'Top'})
              </div>

              {selectedSauces.map((sId) => {
                const s = BUILDER_OPTIONS.find((o) => o.id === sId);
                return (
                  <div
                    key={sId}
                    style={{ backgroundColor: s?.color || '#C44E36' }}
                    className="w-[92%] h-2 rounded-full opacity-90"
                    title={s?.name}
                  />
                );
              })}

              {selectedCheeses.map((cId) => {
                const c = BUILDER_OPTIONS.find((o) => o.id === cId);
                return (
                  <div
                    key={cId}
                    style={{ backgroundColor: c?.color || '#FFA500' }}
                    className="w-[95%] h-3 rounded-md border border-amber-300/60 shadow-2xs flex items-center justify-center text-[9px] font-bold text-stone-800"
                  >
                    🧀 {c?.name.split(' ')[0]}
                  </div>
                );
              })}

              {selectedProteins.map((pId) => {
                const p = BUILDER_OPTIONS.find((o) => o.id === pId);
                return (
                  <div
                    key={pId}
                    style={{ backgroundColor: p?.color || '#993D2C' }}
                    className="w-full h-4.5 rounded-md text-white text-[9px] font-bold flex items-center justify-center shadow-2xs"
                  >
                    🥩 {p?.name.slice(0, 20)}
                  </div>
                );
              })}

              {selectedVeggies.map((vId) => {
                const v = BUILDER_OPTIONS.find((o) => o.id === vId);
                return (
                  <div
                    key={vId}
                    style={{ backgroundColor: v?.color || '#3A7D44' }}
                    className="w-[96%] h-2.5 rounded-full text-white text-[8px] font-bold flex items-center justify-center"
                  >
                    🥬 {v?.name.split(' ')[0]}
                  </div>
                );
              })}

              <div className="w-full h-5 rounded-b-lg bg-[#D99B52] border border-[#B37B39] shadow-2xs flex items-center justify-center text-[10px] font-bold text-[#4B2F10]">
                🍞 {lang === 'ro' ? 'Baza pâinii' : lang === 'it' ? 'Base piatto' : 'Bottom bread'}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-6 sm:gap-8 items-start">
          {/* LEFT: Interactive Options Picker (7 cols on desktop) */}
          <div className="lg:col-span-7 space-y-4 sm:space-y-6">
            {/* Step 1: Bread */}
            <div className="bg-white p-4 sm:p-6 rounded-2xl border border-[#E7DDCE] shadow-2xs">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
                <h3 className="font-bold text-sm sm:text-base text-[#241813] flex items-center gap-2">
                  <span className="w-5 h-5 sm:w-6 sm:h-6 rounded-full bg-[#C44E36] text-white text-xs flex items-center justify-center font-bold shrink-0">
                    1
                  </span>
                  <span>{lang === 'ro' ? 'Alege pâinea / baza' : lang === 'it' ? 'Scegli la base / pane' : 'Select bread base'}</span>
                </h3>
                <span className="text-[11px] text-[#8C7E74]">
                  {lang === 'ro' ? '1 opțiune' : lang === 'it' ? '1 opzione' : '1 choice'}
                </span>
              </div>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-2 sm:gap-2.5">
                {breads.map((bread) => {
                  const isSelected = selectedBread === bread.id;
                  const optName = lang === 'ro' ? bread.name : lang === 'it' ? (bread.nameIt || bread.name) : bread.nameEn;
                  return (
                    <button
                      key={bread.id}
                      id={`builder-bread-${bread.id}`}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setSelectedBread(bread.id)}
                      className={`p-3 rounded-xl border text-left flex items-center justify-between transition-all cursor-pointer min-h-[44px] ${
                        isSelected
                          ? 'bg-[#C44E36]/10 border-[#C44E36] text-[#241813] font-bold shadow-2xs'
                          : 'bg-[#FAF7F2] border-[#E7DDCE] text-[#4A3B32] hover:bg-white'
                      }`}
                    >
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <div
                          className={`w-4 h-4 rounded-full border flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 ${
                            isSelected
                              ? 'border-[#C44E36] bg-[#C44E36] text-white'
                              : 'border-stone-300 bg-white'
                          }`}
                        >
                          {isSelected && <Check className="w-2.5 h-2.5" />}
                        </div>
                        <div>
                          <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm">{optName}</p>
                          <span className="text-[10px] sm:text-[11px] text-[#8C7E74]">{bread.weight}g</span>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                      <span className="font-bold text-xs text-[#C44E36] shrink-0 ml-2">
                        {bread.price} RON
                      </span>
                    </button>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Step 2: Proteins */}
            <div className="bg-white p-4 sm:p-6 rounded-2xl border border-[#E7DDCE] shadow-2xs">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
                <h3 className="font-bold text-sm sm:text-base text-[#241813] flex items-center gap-2">
                  <span className="w-5 h-5 sm:w-6 sm:h-6 rounded-full bg-[#C44E36] text-white text-xs flex items-center justify-center font-bold shrink-0">
                    2
                  </span>
                  <span>{lang === 'ro' ? 'Carne & proteine' : lang === 'it' ? 'Salumi & carni' : 'Proteins & meats'}</span>
                </h3>
                <span className="text-[11px] text-[#8C7E74]">
                  {lang === 'ro' ? 'Selecție multiplă' : lang === 'it' ? 'Scelta multipla' : 'Multiple choices'}
                </span>
              </div>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-2 sm:gap-2.5">
                {proteins.map((p) => {
                  const isSelected = selectedProteins.includes(p.id);
                  const optName = lang === 'ro' ? p.name : lang === 'it' ? (p.nameIt || p.name) : p.nameEn;
                  return (
                    <button
                      key={p.id}
                      id={`builder-protein-${p.id}`}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => toggleArrayItem(selectedProteins, setSelectedProteins, p.id)}
                      className={`p-3 rounded-xl border text-left flex items-center justify-between transition-all cursor-pointer min-h-[44px] ${
                        isSelected
                          ? 'bg-[#C44E36]/10 border-[#C44E36] text-[#241813] font-bold shadow-2xs'
                          : 'bg-[#FAF7F2] border-[#E7DDCE] text-[#4A3B32] hover:bg-white'
                      }`}
                    >
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <div
                          className={`w-4 h-4 rounded-md border flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 ${
                            isSelected
                              ? 'border-[#C44E36] bg-[#C44E36] text-white'
                              : 'border-stone-300 bg-white'
                          }`}
                        >
                          {isSelected && <Check className="w-2.5 h-2.5" />}
                        </div>
                        <div>
                          <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm">{optName}</p>
                          <span className="text-[10px] sm:text-[11px] text-[#8C7E74]">{p.weight}g</span>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                      <span className="font-bold text-xs text-[#C44E36] shrink-0 ml-2">
                        +{p.price} RON
                      </span>
                    </button>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Step 3: Cheeses */}
            <div className="bg-white p-4 sm:p-6 rounded-2xl border border-[#E7DDCE] shadow-2xs">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
                <h3 className="font-bold text-sm sm:text-base text-[#241813] flex items-center gap-2">

              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-2 sm:gap-2.5">

                  return (

                      className={`p-3 rounded-xl border text-left flex items-center justify-between transition-all cursor-pointer min-h-[44px] ${

                          ? 'bg-[#C44E36]/10 border-[#C44E36] text-[#241813] font-bold shadow-2xs'
                          : 'bg-[#FAF7F2] border-[#E7DDCE] text-[#4A3B32] hover:bg-white'
                      }`}

                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">

                              : 'border-stone-300 bg-white'

              <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-3 sm:pb-4 border-b border-[#E7DDCE] mb-4 sm:mb-5">

                >

                  <span>{lang === 'ro' ? 'Resetează' : lang === 'it' ? 'Reimposta' : 'Reset'}</span>
                </button>
              </div>

              {/* Name your sandwich */}
              <div className="mb-4 sm:mb-5">

                <input
                  type="text"

                  className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 text-xs sm:text-sm bg-[#FAF7F2] border border-[#E7DDCE] rounded-xl font-bold text-[#241813] focus:outline-none focus:border-[#C44E36] min-h-[44px]"
                />
              </div>

              {/* Visual Sandwich Stacker Graphic */}
              <div className="bg-[#FAF7F2] p-3 sm:p-4 rounded-2xl border border-[#E7DDCE] mb-4 sm:mb-6 flex flex-col items-center">
                <p className="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider font-bold text-[#8C7E74] mb-2">
                  {lang === 'ro' ? 'Stratificare reală (secțiune)' : lang === 'it' ? 'Stratificazione reale' : 'Live layer stacking'}
                </p>

                <div className="w-full max-w-[240px] flex flex-col gap-1.5 items-center py-2">
                  <div className="w-full h-7 rounded-t-xl bg-[#D99B52] border border-[#B37B39] shadow-2xs flex items-center justify-center text-[11px] font-bold text-[#4B2F10] tracking-wide">
                    🍞 {BUILDER_OPTIONS.find((b) => b.id === selectedBread)?.name.split(' ')[0] || 'Pâine'} ({lang === 'ro' ? 'Capac' : lang === 'it' ? 'Sopra' : 'Top'})
                  </div>

                  {selectedSauces.map((sId) => {
                    const s = BUILDER_OPTIONS.find((o) => o.id === sId);
                    return (
                      <div
                        key={sId}
                        style={{ backgroundColor: s?.color || '#C44E36' }}
                        className="w-[92%] h-2.5 rounded-full opacity-90 transition-all duration-300"
                        title={s?.name}
                      />
                    );
                  })}

                  {selectedCheeses.map((cId) => {
                    const c = BUILDER_OPTIONS.find((o) => o.id === cId);
                    return (
                      <div
                        key={cId}
                        style={{ backgroundColor: c?.color || '#FFA500' }}
                        className="w-[95%] h-3.5 rounded-md border border-amber-300/60 shadow-2xs flex items-center justify-center text-[10px] font-bold text-stone-800"
                      >
                        🧀 {c?.name.split(' ')[0]}
                      </div>
                    );
                  })}

                  {selectedProteins.map((pId) => {
                    const p = BUILDER_OPTIONS.find((o) => o.id === pId);
                    return (
                      <div
                        key={pId}
                        style={{ backgroundColor: p?.color || '#993D2C' }}
                        className="w-full h-5 rounded-md text-white text-[10px] font-bold flex items-center justify-center shadow-2xs"
                      >
                        🥩 {p?.name.slice(0, 22)}...
                      </div>
                    );
                  })}

                  {selectedVeggies.map((vId) => {
                    const v = BUILDER_OPTIONS.find((o) => o.id === vId);
                    return (
                      <div
                        key={vId}
                        style={{ backgroundColor: v?.color || '#3A7D44' }}
                        className="w-[96%] h-3 rounded-full text-white text-[9px] font-bold flex items-center justify-center"
                      >
                        🥬 {v?.name.split(' ')[0]}
                      </div>
                    );
                  })}

                  <div className="w-full h-6 rounded-b-xl bg-[#D99B52] border border-[#B37B39] shadow-2xs flex items-center justify-center text-[11px] font-bold text-[#4B2F10] tracking-wide">
                    🍞 {lang === 'ro' ? 'Baza pâinii' : lang === 'it' ? 'Base piatto' : 'Bottom bread'}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Weight & Price Metrics */}
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2.5 sm:gap-3 mb-4 sm:mb-6">
                <div className="p-3 bg-[#FAF7F2] rounded-xl border border-[#E7DDCE] text-center">
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-[#8C7E74] font-medium">
                    {lang === 'ro' ? 'Gramaj total' : lang === 'it' ? 'Peso totale' : 'Total weight'}
                  </p>
                  <p className="font-serif text-xl sm:text-2xl font-bold text-[#241813]">
                    {totalGrams} <span className="text-xs font-sans font-normal">G</span>
                  </p>
                </div>
                <div className="p-3 bg-[#FAF7F2] rounded-xl border border-[#E7DDCE] text-center">
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-[#8C7E74] font-medium">
                    {lang === 'ro' ? 'Preț total' : lang === 'it' ? 'Prezzo totale' : 'Total price'}
                  </p>
                  <p className="font-serif text-xl sm:text-2xl font-bold text-[#C44E36]">
                    {totalPrice} <span className="text-xs font-sans font-normal">RON</span>
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Action: Add to Cart */}
              <button
                id="builder-add-cart-btn"
                onClick={handleCreateAndAddToCart}
                className="w-full py-3.5 px-6 bg-[#C44E36] hover:bg-[#A63B25] active:scale-98 text-white font-bold rounded-2xl shadow-lg transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-2 text-sm sm:text-base cursor-pointer min-h-[48px]"
              >
                <ShoppingBag className="w-4 h-4" />
                <span>
                  {lang === 'ro' ? 'Comandă creația ta' : lang === 'it' ? 'Ordina il tuo panino' : 'Order your panini'} ({totalPrice} RON)
                </span>
              </button>

              <p className="text-center text-[11px] text-[#8C7E74] mt-2.5">
                {lang === 'ro'
                  ? 'Pregătit manual pe loc la Sandwichef Craiova, Str. Amaradia 29'
                  : lang === 'it'
                  ? 'Preparato fresco sul momento da Sandwichef Craiova, Via Amaradia 29'
                  : 'Crafted to order at Sandwichef Craiova'}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
};

=== FILE: src/components/CateringSection.tsx ===
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Sparkles, Users, CheckCircle2, Phone, Mail, MessageSquare, Calendar, ArrowRight, Building } from 'lucide-react';
import { Language } from '../types';
import { CATERING_PACKAGES } from '../data/menuData';
import { handleSafeImageError, FALLBACK_IMAGES } from '../utils/imageFallback';

interface CateringSectionProps {
  lang: Language;
}

export const CateringSection: React.FC<CateringSectionProps> = ({ lang }) => {
  const [headcount, setHeadcount] = useState<number>(20);
  const [selectedPackageId, setSelectedPackageId] = useState<string>('pack-corporate');
  const [inquiryName, setInquiryName] = useState('');
  const [inquiryPhone, setInquiryPhone] = useState('');
  const [inquiryDate, setInquiryDate] = useState('');
  const [inquiryNotes, setInquiryNotes] = useState('');
  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = useState(false);

  const activePackage = CATERING_PACKAGES.find((p) => p.id === selectedPackageId) || CATERING_PACKAGES[0];
  const estimatedCost = headcount * activePackage.pricePerPerson;
  const estimatedPlatters = Math.ceil(headcount / 5);

  const handleWhatsAppInquiry = () => {
    const pkgName = lang === 'ro' ? activePackage.title : lang === 'it' ? (activePackage.titleIt || activePackage.title) : activePackage.titleEn;
    const text = encodeURIComponent(
      lang === 'ro'
        ? `Salut Sandwichef Craiova! Doresc o ofertă de catering pentru ${headcount} persoane.\n` +
          `Pachet selectat: ${pkgName}\n` +
          `Data eveniment: ${inquiryDate || 'de stabilit'}\n` +
          `Nume contact: ${inquiryName || 'Client'}\n` +
          `Telefon: ${inquiryPhone || 'Nespecificat'}\n` +
          `Mențiuni: ${inquiryNotes || 'Niciuna'}\n` +
          `Buget estimativ: ~${estimatedCost} RON`
        : lang === 'it'
        ? `Ciao Sandwichef Craiova! Vorrei un preventivo di catering per ${headcount} persone.\n` +
          `Pacchetto scelto: ${pkgName}\n` +
          `Data evento: ${inquiryDate || 'da definire'}\n` +
          `Nome contatto: ${inquiryName || 'Cliente'}\n` +
          `Telefono: ${inquiryPhone || 'Non specificato'}\n` +
          `Note: ${inquiryNotes || 'Nessuna'}\n` +
          `Budget stimato: ~${estimatedCost} RON`
        : `Hello Sandwichef Craiova! I would like a catering quote for ${headcount} guests.\n` +
          `Package: ${pkgName}\n` +
          `Event date: ${inquiryDate || 'TBD'}\n` +
          `Contact name: ${inquiryName || 'Client'}\n` +
          `Phone: ${inquiryPhone || 'Not specified'}\n` +
          `Notes: ${inquiryNotes || 'None'}\n` +
          `Estimated budget: ~${estimatedCost} RON`
    );
    window.open(`https://wa.me/40769409493?text=${text}`, '_blank');
  };

  const handleEmailInquiry = () => {
    const pkgName = lang === 'ro' ? activePackage.title : lang === 'it' ? (activePackage.titleIt || activePackage.title) : activePackage.titleEn;
    const subject = encodeURIComponent(
      lang === 'ro'
        ? `Solicitare Catering Sandwichef - ${headcount} persoane - ${inquiryName || 'Eveniment'}`
        : lang === 'it'
        ? `Richiesta Catering Sandwichef - ${headcount} persone - ${inquiryName || 'Evento'}`
        : `Catering Inquiry Sandwichef - ${headcount} guests - ${inquiryName || 'Event'}`
    );
    const body = encodeURIComponent(
      lang === 'ro'
        ? `Bună ziua echipa Sandwichef Craiova,\n\n` +
          `Aș dori o ofertă detaliată pentru un eveniment de catering:\n\n` +
          `• Număr de persoane: ${headcount}\n` +
          `• Pachet de interes: ${pkgName}\n` +
          `• Data eveniment: ${inquiryDate || 'În curând'}\n` +
          `• Nume: ${inquiryName}\n` +
          `• Telefon: ${inquiryPhone}\n` +
          `• Mențiuni / Preferințe: ${inquiryNotes || 'Fără mențiuni speciale'}\n\n` +
          `Vă mulțumesc!`
        : lang === 'it'
        ? `Gentile team Sandwichef Craiova,\n\n` +
          `Vorrei richiedere un preventivo per un evento di catering:\n\n` +
          `• Numero di persone: ${headcount}\n` +
          `• Pacchetto di interesse: ${pkgName}\n` +
          `• Data evento: ${inquiryDate || 'A breve'}\n` +
          `• Nome: ${inquiryName}\n` +
          `• Telefono: ${inquiryPhone}\n` +
          `• Note / Preferenze: ${inquiryNotes || 'Nessuna preferenza speciale'}\n\n` +
          `Grazie cordialmente!`
        : `Hello Sandwichef Craiova team,\n\n` +
          `I would like a detailed catering quote:\n\n` +
          `• Number of guests: ${headcount}\n` +
          `• Package: ${pkgName}\n` +
          `• Date: ${inquiryDate || 'Upcoming'}\n` +
          `• Name: ${inquiryName}\n` +
          `• Phone: ${inquiryPhone}\n` +
          `• Notes / Dietary requirements: ${inquiryNotes || 'None'}\n\n` +
          `Thank you!`
    );
    window.location.href = `mailto:sandwichefcraiova@gmail.com?subject=${subject}&body=${body}`;
  };

  return (
    <section id="catering" className="py-20 bg-[#FAF7F2]">
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
        {/* Header Title */}
        <div className="text-center max-w-3xl mx-auto mb-14">
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3.5 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-[#C44E36] bg-[#C44E36]/10 mb-3">
            <Building className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            {lang === 'ro' ? 'Servicii evenimente & birou' : lang === 'it' ? 'Servizi eventi & ufficio' : 'Event & office services'}
          </span>
          <h2 className="font-serif text-3xl sm:text-5xl font-bold text-[#241813] tracking-tight mb-4">
            {lang === 'ro' ? 'Catering Sandwichef Craiova' : lang === 'it' ? 'Catering Sandwichef Craiova' : 'Sandwichef Catering Craiova'}
          </h2>
          <p className="text-base sm:text-lg text-[#6B5E55] leading-relaxed">
            {lang === 'ro'
              ? 'Partenerul tău de încredere pentru pauze de prânz corporate, conferințe, team buildinguri și petreceri private. Livrăm promptitudine și savoare toscană.'
              : lang === 'it'
              ? 'Il tuo partner di fiducia per pause pranzo aziendali, conferenze, team building e feste private. Puntualità e autentico gusto toscano.'
              : 'Your trusted partner for corporate lunches, conferences, team buildings, and private celebrations with authentic taste.'}
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Feature Banner with real photo */}
        <div className="relative rounded-3xl overflow-hidden mb-16 shadow-xl border border-[#E7DDCE]">
          <div className="h-64 sm:h-80 w-full relative">
            <img
              src="/images/swc-catering.jpg"
              alt="Platouri Catering Sandwichef"
              className="w-full h-full object-cover"
              onError={(e) => {
                handleSafeImageError(e, FALLBACK_IMAGES.catering);
              }}
            />
            <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-r from-[#241813]/90 via-[#241813]/65 to-transparent flex items-center p-6 sm:p-12">
              <div className="max-w-xl text-white">
                <span className="bg-[#F0BE2C] text-[#241813] text-xs font-bold px-3 py-1 rounded-full uppercase tracking-wider mb-3 inline-block">
                  {lang === 'ro' ? 'Comenzi cu 24h înainte' : lang === 'it' ? 'Ordini con 24h di anticipo' : 'Order 24h in advance'}
                </span>
                <h3 className="font-serif text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-bold mb-3">
                  {lang === 'ro'
                    ? 'Platouri mini-schiacciata & wraps gourmet'
                    : lang === 'it'
                    ? 'Vassoi mini-schiacciata & wraps gourmet'
                    : 'Gourmet mini-schiacciata & wrap platters'}
                </h3>
                <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-stone-200 leading-relaxed">
                  {lang === 'ro'
                    ? 'Fiecare sandwich este porționat elegant pentru sharing ușor. Adaptăm rețetele în funcție de cerințe vegetariene, fără gluten sau fără carne.'

              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Catering Packages Grid */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-6 mb-16">
          {CATERING_PACKAGES.map((pkg) => {

            const pkgTitle = lang === 'ro' ? pkg.title : lang === 'it' ? (pkg.titleIt || pkg.title) : pkg.titleEn;
            const pkgBadge = lang === 'ro' ? pkg.badge : lang === 'it' ? (pkg.badgeIt || pkg.badge) : pkg.badgeEn;
            const pkgDesc = lang === 'ro' ? pkg.description : lang === 'it' ? (pkg.descriptionIt || pkg.description) : pkg.descriptionEn;
            const includesList = lang === 'ro' ? pkg.includes : lang === 'it' ? (pkg.includesIt || pkg.includes) : pkg.includesEn;

            return (

                onClick={() => setSelectedPackageId(pkg.id)}
                className={`bg-white rounded-2xl p-6 border transition-all duration-300 cursor-pointer flex flex-col justify-between hover:shadow-lg ${
                  isSelected
                    ? 'border-[#C44E36] ring-2 ring-[#C44E36]/30 shadow-md -translate-y-1'

                <span className="font-bold text-sm text-[#241813]">
                  ~{estimatedPlatters} {lang === 'ro' ? 'platouri mari' : lang === 'it' ? 'vassoi grandi' : 'large platters'}

                <span className="text-[11px] text-[#8C7E74] block">
                  {lang === 'ro' ? 'Pachet selectat' : lang === 'it' ? 'Pacchetto scelto' : 'Selected package'}
                </span>
                <span className="font-bold text-xs text-[#241813] truncate block">
                  {lang === 'ro' ? activePackage.title : lang === 'it' ? (activePackage.titleIt || activePackage.title) : activePackage.titleEn}

                </label>
                <input

                  className="w-full px-3.5 py-2 text-xs sm:text-sm bg-[#FAF7F2] border border-[#E7DDCE] rounded-xl focus:outline-none focus:border-[#C44E36]"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-[#8C7E74] uppercase mb-1">

              </label>
              <textarea
                rows={2}
                value={inquiryNotes}
                onChange={(e) => setInquiryNotes(e.target.value)}
                placeholder={lang === 'ro' ? 'Ex: 5 meniuri vegetariene, livrare la sediul din Craiova la ora 12:00 etc.' : lang === 'it' ? 'Es: 5 opzioni vegetariane, consegna ore 12:00...' : 'e.g. 5 vegetarian options, delivery at 12:00...'}
                className="w-full px-3.5 py-2 text-xs sm:text-sm bg-[#FAF7F2] border border-[#E7DDCE] rounded-xl focus:outline-none focus:border-[#C44E36]"
              />
            </div>

            >

                {lang === 'ro'

                  : `Send on WhatsApp (~${estimatedCost} RON)`}

                {lang === 'ro' ? 'Trimite solicitare prin email' : lang === 'it' ? 'Invia richiesta via email' : 'Send request via email'}
              </span>
            </button>

            <a
              id="catering-call-btn"
              href="tel:0735468253"
              className="py-3.5 px-6 bg-[#FAF7F2] hover:bg-[#F0EAE1] text-[#241813] border border-[#E7DDCE] font-bold rounded-2xl transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-2 text-sm"
            >
              <Phone className="w-4 h-4 text-[#C44E36]" />
              <span>{lang === 'ro' ? 'Sună la 0735 468 253' : lang === 'it' ? 'Chiama 0735 468 253' : 'Call 0735 468 253'}</span>
            </a>
          </div>

          <p className="text-center text-xs text-[#8C7E74] mt-4">
            {lang === 'ro'
              ? 'Sandwichef Craiova răspunde în maxim 2 ore. Livrare gratuită în Craiova pentru comenzi peste 300 RON.'
              : lang === 'it'
              ? 'Sandwichef Craiova risponde entro 2 ore. Consegna gratuita a Craiova per ordini superiori a 300 RON.'
              : 'Sandwichef Craiova replies within 2 hours. Free delivery in Craiova on orders over 300 RON.'}
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
};

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { MapPin, Phone, Clock, Mail, Navigation, ExternalLink, ShieldCheck, Share2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { Language } from '../types';
import { getStoreStatus } from '../utils/status';

interface LocationContactSectionProps {
  lang: Language;
}

export const LocationContactSection: React.FC<LocationContactSectionProps> = ({ lang }) => {
  const [storeStatus, setStoreStatus] = useState(getStoreStatus());
  const [mapInteractive, setMapInteractive] = useState(false);
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const update = () => setStoreStatus(getStoreStatus());
    update();
    const interval = setInterval(update, 30000);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, []);

  const handleShareAddress = async () => {
    if (navigator.share) {
      try {
        await navigator.share({
          title: 'Sandwichef Craiova',
          text: 'Strada Amaradia, Nr. 29, Craiova, Dolj - Panini Gourmet & Sandwichuri Artizanale',
          url: 'https://maps.app.goo.gl/UqDviip6A3CN9qXP7',
        });
      } catch (e) {
        // cancelled
      }
    } else {
      navigator.clipboard.writeText('Strada Amaradia, Nr. 29, Craiova, Dolj');
      setCopied(true);
      setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000);
    }
  };

  const scheduleRows = [
    { dayRo: 'Luni', dayEn: 'Monday', dayIt: 'Lunedì', hours: '09:30 - 17:30', hoursIt: '09:30 - 17:30', dayIndex: 1 },
    { dayRo: 'Marți', dayEn: 'Tuesday', dayIt: 'Martedì', hours: '09:30 - 17:30', hoursIt: '09:30 - 17:30', dayIndex: 2 },
    { dayRo: 'Miercuri', dayEn: 'Wednesday', dayIt: 'Mercoledì', hours: '09:30 - 17:30', hoursIt: '09:30 - 17:30', dayIndex: 3 },
    { dayRo: 'Joi', dayEn: 'Thursday', dayIt: 'Giovedì', hours: '09:30 - 17:30', hoursIt: '09:30 - 17:30', dayIndex: 4 },
    { dayRo: 'Vineri', dayEn: 'Friday', dayIt: 'Venerdì', hours: '09:30 - 17:30', hoursIt: '09:30 - 17:30', dayIndex: 5 },
    { dayRo: 'Sâmbătă', dayEn: 'Saturday', dayIt: 'Sabato', hours: 'Închis (Catering la cerere)', hoursIt: 'Chiuso (Catering su richiesta)', dayIndex: 6 },
    { dayRo: 'Duminică', dayEn: 'Sunday', dayIt: 'Domenica', hours: 'Închis (Catering la cerere)', hoursIt: 'Chiuso (Catering su richiesta)', dayIndex: 0 },
  ];

  const todayIndex = new Date().getDay();

  return (
    <section id="location" className="py-12 sm:py-20 bg-[#FAF7F2]">
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-3 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="text-center max-w-2xl mx-auto mb-6 sm:mb-12">
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-[11px] sm:text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-[#C44E36] bg-[#C44E36]/10 mb-2 sm:mb-3">
            <MapPin className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            {lang === 'ro' ? 'Vino să ne cunoști' : lang === 'it' ? 'Vieni a trovarci' : 'Visit us in Craiova'}
          </span>
          <h2 className="font-serif text-2xl sm:text-4xl md:text-5xl font-bold text-[#241813] tracking-tight mb-2 sm:mb-3">
            {lang === 'ro' ? 'Locație & program Sandwichef' : lang === 'it' ? 'Posizione & orari Sandwichef' : 'Location & store hours'}
          </h2>
          <p className="text-xs sm:text-base text-[#6B5E55] leading-relaxed px-2">
            {lang === 'ro'
              ? 'Te așteptăm în Craiova pe Strada Amaradia 29 pentru sandwichuri proaspăt rumenite sau comandă prin livrare.'
              : lang === 'it'
              ? 'Ti aspettiamo a Craiova in Strada Amaradia 29 per panini caldi e fragranti o ordina a domicilio.'
              : 'Visit our sandwich shop on Amaradia Street 29, Craiova, or order delivery.'}
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Quick 1-Tap Mobile Navigation Dial Row */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-2 sm:gap-3 mb-6">
          <a
            href="tel:0735468253"
            className="p-3 rounded-2xl bg-white border border-[#E7DDCE] shadow-2xs hover:border-[#C44E36] flex items-center gap-2.5 transition-all text-left min-h-[48px]"
          >
            <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-[#C44E36]/10 text-[#C44E36] flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
              <Phone className="w-4 h-4" />
            </div>
            <div className="min-w-0">
              <p className="text-[10px] text-[#8C7E74] uppercase font-bold">
                {lang === 'ro' ? 'Apel direct' : lang === 'it' ? 'Chiamata' : 'Direct call'}
              </p>
              <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-[#241813] truncate">0735 468 253</p>
            </div>
          </a>

          <a
            href="https://maps.app.goo.gl/UqDviip6A3CN9qXP7"
            target="_blank"
            rel="noreferrer"
            className="p-3 rounded-2xl bg-white border border-[#E7DDCE] shadow-2xs hover:border-[#C44E36] flex items-center gap-2.5 transition-all text-left min-h-[48px]"

              <p className="text-[10px] text-[#8C7E74] uppercase font-bold">Google Maps</p>
              <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-[#241813] truncate">
                {lang === 'ro' ? 'Deschide harta' : lang === 'it' ? 'Apri mappa' : 'Open map'}
              </p>
            </div>

                title="Facebook"
              >
                fb
              </a>
              <a
                href="https://www.tiktok.com/@sandwichef1"
                target="_blank"

              >
                tt
              </a>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Quick Links */}
          <div>
            <h4 className="font-serif text-base font-bold text-white mb-4">

              </li>

                  onClick={() => onNavigateTo('story')}

                </button>

              </li>
              {onOpenApiKeySettings && (
                <li className="pt-1">
                  <button
                    onClick={onOpenApiKeySettings}
                    className="text-[11px] text-stone-400 hover:text-[#F0BE2C] transition-colors cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-1.5"
                  >
                    <span>🔑</span>

                  0735 468 253
                </a>
              </li>

                <Mail className="w-4 h-4 text-[#C44E36] shrink-0" />

                  className="hover:text-white"

                  sandwichefcraiova@gmail.com
                </a>
              </li>
              <li className="flex items-start gap-2.5">

                href="https://wolt.com/ro/rou/craiova/restaurant/sandwichef-68dfdfbd3a8557c375c011d6"

              href="https://anpc.ro/ce-este-sal/"

=== FILE: src/App.tsx ===
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Navbar } from './components/Navbar';
import { HeroSection } from './components/HeroSection';
import { StorySection } from './components/StorySection';
import { MenuSection } from './components/MenuSection';
import { SandwichLab } from './components/SandwichLab';
import { CateringSection } from './components/CateringSection';
import { LocationContactSection } from './components/LocationContactSection';
import { Footer } from './components/Footer';
import { CartDrawer } from './components/CartDrawer';
import { ItemDetailModal } from './components/ItemDetailModal';
import { ApiKeyModal } from './components/ApiKeyModal';
import { MobileBottomBar } from './components/MobileBottomBar';
import { CartItem, MenuItem, Language } from './types';
import { MENU_ITEMS } from './data/menuData';
import { Check, ShoppingBag, ArrowLeft, Sparkles, Utensils, ArrowRight, Star } from 'lucide-react';
import { handleSafeImageError, FALLBACK_IMAGES } from './utils/imageFallback';

export type AppPage = 'home' | 'menu' | 'builder' | 'catering' | 'location';

const getPageFromHash = (): AppPage => {
  const hash = window.location.hash.replace('#', '').replace('/', '').toLowerCase();
  if (hash === 'menu' || hash === 'meniu') return 'menu';
  if (hash === 'builder' || hash === 'chef-lab' || hash === 'cheflab') return 'builder';
  if (hash === 'catering') return 'catering';
  if (hash === 'location' || hash === 'locatie') return 'location';
  return 'home';
};

export default function App() {
  const [currentPage, setCurrentPage] = useState<AppPage>(getPageFromHash);
  const [lang, setLang] = useState<Language>('ro');
  const [cartItems, setCartItems] = useState<CartItem[]>([]);
  const [isCartOpen, setIsCartOpen] = useState(false);
  const [activeDetailItem, setActiveDetailItem] = useState<MenuItem | null>(null);
  const [isApiKeyModalOpen, setIsApiKeyModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [toastMessage, setToastMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Sync state with browser back/forward buttons
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleHashChange = () => {
      const page = getPageFromHash();
      setCurrentPage(page);
      window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
    };
    window.addEventListener('hashchange', handleHashChange);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('hashchange', handleHashChange);
  }, []);

  const showToast = (msg: string) => {
    setToastMessage(msg);
    setTimeout(() => {
      setToastMessage(null);
    }, 2800);
  };

  // Route to page and update URL hash
  const handleNavigateTo = (id: string) => {
    let targetPage: AppPage = 'home';
    if (id === 'menu') targetPage = 'menu';
    else if (id === 'builder' || id === 'cheflab' || id === 'chef-lab') targetPage = 'builder';
    else if (id === 'catering') targetPage = 'catering';
    else if (id === 'location') targetPage = 'location';
    else if (id === 'home') targetPage = 'home';

    setCurrentPage(targetPage);
    const newHash = targetPage === 'home' ? '' : targetPage === 'builder' ? '#chef-lab' : `#${targetPage}`;
    
    if (window.location.hash !== newHash) {
      if (newHash) {
        window.history.pushState(null, '', newHash);
      } else {
        window.history.pushState(null, '', window.location.pathname);
      }
    }
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
  };

  // Add standard or personalized item from modal
  const handleAddToCartFromModal = (
    item: MenuItem,
    quantity: number,
    removed: string[],
    extras: string[],
    notes: string
  ) => {
    const newCartItem: CartItem = {
      id: `${item.id}-${Date.now()}`,
      menuItem: item,
      quantity,
      selectedExtras: extras,
      removedIngredients: removed,
      specialInstructions: notes,
    };

    setCartItems((prev) => [...prev, newCartItem]);
    showToast(
      lang === 'ro'
        ? `Adăugat: ${quantity}x ${item.name}`
        : lang === 'it'
        ? `Aggiunto: ${quantity}x ${item.nameIt || item.name}`
        : `Added: ${quantity}x ${item.nameEn || item.name}`
    );
  };

  // Quick add button on card
  const handleQuickAdd = (item: MenuItem, e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    const existingIndex = cartItems.findIndex(
      (ci) =>
        ci.menuItem.id === item.id &&
        (!ci.selectedExtras || ci.selectedExtras.length === 0) &&
        (!ci.removedIngredients || ci.removedIngredients.length === 0)
    );

    if (existingIndex > -1) {
      setCartItems((prev) =>
        prev.map((ci, idx) =>
          idx === existingIndex ? { ...ci, quantity: ci.quantity + 1 } : ci
        )
      );
    } else {
      const newCartItem: CartItem = {
        id: `${item.id}-${Date.now()}`,
        menuItem: item,
        quantity: 1,
      };
      setCartItems((prev) => [...prev, newCartItem]);
    }

    showToast(
      lang === 'ro'
        ? `1x ${item.name} adăugat în coș!`
        : lang === 'it'
        ? `1x ${item.nameIt || item.name} aggiunto al carrello!`
        : `1x ${item.nameEn || item.name} added to cart!`
    );
  };

  // Custom sandwich from Chef Lab
  const handleAddCustomSandwich = (
    customItem: MenuItem,
    details: {
      bread: string;
      proteins: string[];
      cheeses: string[];
      veggies: string[];
      sauces: string[];
      totalWeight: number;
    }
  ) => {
    const newCartItem: CartItem = {
      id: customItem.id,
      menuItem: customItem,
      quantity: 1,
      specialInstructions: `Bază: ${details.bread} | Proteine: ${details.proteins.join(', ')} | Brânză: ${details.cheeses.join(', ')} | Veg: ${details.veggies.join(', ')} | Sos: ${details.sauces.join(', ')}`,
      customSandwichDetails: details,
    };

    setCartItems((prev) => [...prev, newCartItem]);
    setIsCartOpen(true);
    showToast(
      lang === 'ro'
        ? `Creația ta "${customItem.name}" a fost adăugată!`
        : lang === 'it'
        ? `La tua creazione "${customItem.name}" è stata aggiunta!`
        : `Your custom "${customItem.name}" was added!`
    );
  };

  // Quantity updates
  const handleUpdateQuantity = (id: string, delta: number) => {
    setCartItems((prev) =>
      prev
        .map((item) => {
          if (item.id === id) {
            const newQ = item.quantity + delta;
            return newQ > 0 ? { ...item, quantity: newQ } : null;
          }
          return item;
        })
        .filter(Boolean) as CartItem[]
    );
  };

  const handleRemoveItem = (id: string) => {
    setCartItems((prev) => prev.filter((item) => item.id !== id));
  };

  const handleClearCart = () => {
    setCartItems([]);
  };

  const totalCartCount = cartItems.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.quantity, 0);
  const totalCartPrice = cartItems.reduce(
    (sum, item) => sum + item.menuItem.price * item.quantity,
    0
  );

  // Featured popular sandwiches for the visual photo showcase on homepage
  const featuredSandwiches = [
    MENU_ITEMS.find((item) => item.id === 'swc-1'), // Morning Sandwichef Bacon
    MENU_ITEMS.find((item) => item.id === 'swc-2'), // Bagheta fresh
    MENU_ITEMS.find((item) => item.id === 'swc-7'), // Smashed sandwich vită
    MENU_ITEMS.find((item) => item.id === 'swc-10'), // Panini Mamamia
  ].filter(Boolean) as MenuItem[];

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#FAF7F2] text-[#241813] flex flex-col font-sans selection:bg-[#C44E36] selection:text-white pb-16 md:pb-0">
      {/* Sticky Top Navbar */}
      <Navbar
        cartCount={totalCartCount}
        onOpenCart={() => setIsCartOpen(true)}
        onNavigateTo={handleNavigateTo}
        activeTab={currentPage}
        setActiveTab={(tab) => handleNavigateTo(tab)}
        lang={lang}
        setLang={setLang}
        onToggleLang={() => setLang(lang === 'ro' ? 'en' : lang === 'en' ? 'it' : 'ro')}
      />

      {/* Main Content Flow - Switch between Dedicated Pages */}
      <main className="flex-1">
        {/* ==================================================== */}
        {/* PAGE 1: DEDICATED MENU PAGE                          */}
        {/* ==================================================== */}
        {currentPage === 'menu' && (
          <div id="page-menu" className="pt-16 sm:pt-20">
            {/* Page Header & Breadcrumb Bar */}
            <div className="bg-[#F0EAE1] border-b border-[#E4D9CA] py-3.5 px-4 sm:px-6">
              <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto flex items-center gap-2 text-xs sm:text-sm text-[#6B5E55]">
                <button
                  id="menu-back-to-home-btn"
                  onClick={() => handleNavigateTo('home')}
                  className="hover:text-[#C44E36] font-semibold flex items-center gap-1 cursor-pointer transition-colors"
                >
                  <ArrowLeft className="w-4 h-4" />
                  <span>{lang === 'ro' ? 'Acasă' : lang === 'it' ? 'Inizio' : 'Home'}</span>
                </button>
                <span>/</span>
                <span className="font-bold text-[#241813]">
                  {lang === 'ro' ? 'Meniu complet' : lang === 'it' ? 'Menu completo' : 'Full menu'}
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Complete Menu Section with live filters and details */}
            <MenuSection
              onSelectItem={(item) => setActiveDetailItem(item)}
              onQuickAdd={handleQuickAdd}
              lang={lang}
            />

            {/* Bottom Recommendation to Catering & Chef Lab */}
            <div className="bg-[#FAF7F2] pb-16 pt-4 px-4 border-t border-[#241813]/10">
              <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                <div className="bg-[#F0EAE1] p-6 rounded-3xl border border-[#E4D9CA] flex flex-col justify-between">
                  <div>
                    <span className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wider font-bold text-[#C44E36]">
                      {lang === 'ro' ? 'Pentru echipe & evenimente' : lang === 'it' ? 'Per team & eventi' : 'For teams & events'}
                    </span>
                    <h3 className="font-serif text-xl font-bold text-[#241813] mt-1 mb-2">
                      {lang === 'ro' ? 'Platouri schiacciata & catering' : lang === 'it' ? 'Taglieri schiacciata & catering' : 'Schiacciata catering platters'}
                    </h3>
                    <p className="text-xs text-[#6B5E55] leading-relaxed mb-4">
                      {lang === 'ro'
                        ? 'Comandă platouri asortate de mini schiacciata pentru birou sau petreceri private.'
                        : lang === 'it'
                        ? 'Ordina taglieri assortiti di mini schiacciate per riunioni o eventi privati.'
                        : 'Order assorted mini Schiacciata platters for meetings and private celebrations.'}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                  <button
                    onClick={() => handleNavigateTo('catering')}
                    className="self-start inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-[#C44E36] hover:underline cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    <span>{lang === 'ro' ? 'Vezi oferta catering' : lang === 'it' ? 'Vedi catering' : 'View catering'}</span>
                    <ArrowRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  </button>
                </div>

                <div className="bg-[#F0EAE1] p-6 rounded-3xl border border-[#E4D9CA] flex flex-col justify-between">
                  <div>
                    <span className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wider font-bold text-[#D97706]">
                      {lang === 'ro' ? 'Creativitate nelimitată' : lang === 'it' ? 'Creatività senza limiti' : 'Unlimited creativity'}
                    </span>
                    <h3 className="font-serif text-xl font-bold text-[#241813] mt-1 mb-2">
                      Chef Lab studio ✨
                    </h3>
                    <p className="text-xs text-[#6B5E55] leading-relaxed mb-4">
                      {lang === 'ro'
                        ? 'Alege carnea, brânza, sosul și legumele preferate pentru un sandwich preparat exact pe gustul tău.'
                        : lang === 'it'
                        ? 'Scegli la tua combinazione preferita di carne, formaggi e salse al momento.'
                        : 'Choose your preferred meats, artisan cheeses, sauces and veggies made to order.'}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                  <button
                    onClick={() => handleNavigateTo('builder')}
                    className="self-start inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-[#C44E36] hover:underline cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    <span>{lang === 'ro' ? 'Deschide Chef Lab' : lang === 'it' ? 'Apri Chef Lab' : 'Open Chef Lab'}</span>
                    <ArrowRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* ==================================================== */}
        {/* PAGE 2: DEDICATED CHEF LAB PAGE                      */}
        {/* ==================================================== */}
        {currentPage === 'builder' && (
          <div id="page-builder" className="pt-16 sm:pt-20">
            {/* Page Header & Breadcrumb Bar */}
            <div className="bg-[#F0EAE1] border-b border-[#E4D9CA] py-3.5 px-4 sm:px-6">
              <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto flex items-center gap-2 text-xs sm:text-sm text-[#6B5E55]">
                <button
                  id="builder-back-to-home-btn"
                  onClick={() => handleNavigateTo('home')}
                  className="hover:text-[#C44E36] font-semibold flex items-center gap-1 cursor-pointer transition-colors"
                >
                  <ArrowLeft className="w-4 h-4" />
                  <span>{lang === 'ro' ? 'Acasă' : lang === 'it' ? 'Inizio' : 'Home'}</span>
                </button>
                <span>/</span>
                <span className="font-bold text-[#241813]">Chef Lab studio ✨</span>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Interactive Custom Sandwich Studio */}
            <SandwichLab
              onAddCustomSandwich={handleAddCustomSandwich}
              lang={lang}
            />
          </div>
        )}

        {/* ==================================================== */}
        {/* PAGE 3: DEDICATED CATERING PAGE                      */}
        {/* ==================================================== */}
        {currentPage === 'catering' && (
          <div id="page-catering" className="pt-16 sm:pt-20">
            {/* Page Header & Breadcrumb Bar */}
            <div className="bg-[#F0EAE1] border-b border-[#E4D9CA] py-3.5 px-4 sm:px-6">
              <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto flex items-center gap-2 text-xs sm:text-sm text-[#6B5E55]">
                <button
                  onClick={() => handleNavigateTo('home')}
                  className="hover:text-[#C44E36] font-semibold flex items-center gap-1 cursor-pointer transition-colors"
                >
                  <ArrowLeft className="w-4 h-4" />
                  <span>{lang === 'ro' ? 'Acasă' : lang === 'it' ? 'Inizio' : 'Home'}</span>
                </button>
                <span>/</span>
                <span className="font-bold text-[#241813]">
                  {lang === 'ro' ? 'Catering evenimente' : lang === 'it' ? 'Catering eventi' : 'Event catering'}
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>

            <CateringSection lang={lang} />
          </div>
        )}

        {/* ==================================================== */}
        {/* PAGE 4: DEDICATED LOCATION PAGE                      */}
        {/* ==================================================== */}
        {currentPage === 'location' && (
          <div id="page-location" className="pt-16 sm:pt-20">
            {/* Page Header & Breadcrumb Bar */}
            <div className="bg-[#F0EAE1] border-b border-[#E4D9CA] py-3.5 px-4 sm:px-6">
              <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto flex items-center gap-2 text-xs sm:text-sm text-[#6B5E55]">
                <button
                  onClick={() => handleNavigateTo('home')}
                  className="hover:text-[#C44E36] font-semibold flex items-center gap-1 cursor-pointer transition-colors"
                >
                  <ArrowLeft className="w-4 h-4" />
                  <span>{lang === 'ro' ? 'Acasă' : lang === 'it' ? 'Inizio' : 'Home'}</span>
                </button>
                <span>/</span>
                <span className="font-bold text-[#241813]">
                  {lang === 'ro' ? 'Locație Craiova & contact' : lang === 'it' ? 'Posizione & contatti' : 'Location & contact'}
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>

            <LocationContactSection lang={lang} />
          </div>
        )}

        {/* ==================================================== */}
        {/* PAGE 5: MAIN HOMEPAGE                                */}
        {/* ==================================================== */}
        {currentPage === 'home' && (
          <div id="page-home">
            {/* Hero Section */}
            <HeroSection
              onExploreMenu={() => handleNavigateTo('menu')}
              onOpenBuilder={() => handleNavigateTo('builder')}
              lang={lang}
            />

            {/* Story & Philosophy Section with 4 Visual Cards */}
            <StorySection onNavigateTo={handleNavigateTo} lang={lang} />

            {/* FEATURED BESTSELLERS TEASER ON HOME */}
            <section className="py-14 sm:py-20 bg-[#FAF7F2] border-t border-[#241813]/10">
              <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
                <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-end justify-between gap-4 mb-8 sm:mb-12">
                  <div>
                    <span className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wider font-bold text-[#C44E36] block mb-1">
                      {lang === 'ro' ? 'Selecția zilei' : lang === 'it' ? 'Selezione del giorno' : 'Daily selection'}
                    </span>
                    <h2 className="font-serif text-2xl sm:text-4xl font-bold text-[#241813]">
                      {lang === 'ro'
                        ? 'Cele mai iubite sandwichuri toscane'
                        : lang === 'it'
                        ? 'I nostri panini più apprezzati'
                        : 'Our most loved Tuscan panini'}
                    </h2>
                  </div>

                  {/* Button to open the full Meniu page */}
                  <button
                    id="home-open-full-menu-btn"
                    onClick={() => handleNavigateTo('menu')}
                    className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-5 py-2.5 rounded-full bg-[#C44E36] hover:bg-[#A63B25] text-white text-sm font-bold shadow-xs hover:shadow-md transition-all cursor-pointer self-start sm:self-auto active:scale-95"
                  >
                    <span>
                      {lang === 'ro'
                        ? 'Deschide meniul complet (19 rețete)'
                        : lang === 'it'
                        ? 'Apri il menu completo (19 ricette)'
                        : 'Open full menu (19 recipes)'}
                    </span>
                    <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
                  </button>
                </div>

                {/* Visual Photo Showcase of Popular Panini (Photos only, click navigates to menu to order) */}
                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-5 sm:gap-6 mb-8">
                  {featuredSandwiches.map((item) => (
                    <div
                      key={item.id}
                      onClick={() => handleNavigateTo('menu')}
                      className="group bg-[#F0EAE1] rounded-3xl overflow-hidden border border-[#E4D9CA] shadow-2xs hover:shadow-lg transition-all duration-300 flex flex-col cursor-pointer transform hover:-translate-y-1"
                    >
                      <div className="relative h-60 sm:h-64 overflow-hidden bg-stone-200">
                        <img
                          src={item.image}
                          alt={item.name}
                          className="w-full h-full object-cover group-hover:scale-108 transition-transform duration-500 ease-out"
                          onError={(e) => {
                            handleSafeImageError(e, FALLBACK_IMAGES.sandwich);
                          }}
                        />
                        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-[#241813]/90 via-[#241813]/30 to-transparent opacity-90 group-hover:opacity-95 transition-opacity" />

                        {/* Top Badges */}
                        <div className="absolute top-3 left-3 bg-[#241813]/80 backdrop-blur-md text-white text-[11px] font-bold px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full flex items-center gap-1 shadow-xs">
                          <Star className="w-3 h-3 text-[#F0BE2C] fill-[#F0BE2C]" />
                          <span>{item.weight}g</span>
                        </div>
                        <div className="absolute top-3 right-3 bg-[#C44E36] text-white text-xs font-extrabold px-3 py-1 rounded-full shadow-xs">
                          {item.price} lei
                        </div>

                        {/* Bottom Content Overlay on the Photo */}
                        <div className="absolute bottom-0 inset-x-0 p-4 text-white">
                          <h3 className="font-serif text-lg font-bold text-white leading-tight mb-1 group-hover:text-[#F0BE2C] transition-colors">
                            {lang === 'ro' ? item.name : lang === 'it' ? item.nameIt || item.name : item.nameEn || item.name}
                          </h3>
                          <p className="text-[11px] text-stone-200 line-clamp-2 leading-relaxed mb-3 opacity-90">
                            {lang === 'ro'
                              ? item.description
                              : lang === 'it'
                              ? item.descriptionIt || item.description
                              : item.descriptionEn || item.description}
                          </p>

                          {/* Subtle prompt indicating click to see in menu */}
                          <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-[#F0BE2C] group-hover:underline">
                            <span>{lang === 'ro' ? 'Vezi în meniu' : lang === 'it' ? 'Vedi nel menu' : 'View in menu'}</span>
                            <ArrowRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5 transition-transform group-hover:translate-x-1" />
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>

                {/* Guidance Banner: Ordering & adding is done inside the full menu */}
                <div className="mb-12 p-4 sm:p-5 rounded-2xl bg-white border border-[#E7DDCE] flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-4 shadow-2xs">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-center sm:text-left">
                    <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-[#C44E36]/10 text-[#C44E36] flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 mx-auto sm:mx-0">
                      <Utensils className="w-5 h-5" />
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-[#241813]">
                        {lang === 'ro'
                          ? 'Vrei să comanzi? Deschide meniul complet'
                          : lang === 'it'
                          ? 'Vuoi ordinare? Apri il menu completo'
                          : 'Ready to order? Open the full menu'}
                      </p>
                      <p className="text-[11px] sm:text-xs text-[#8C7E74]">
                        {lang === 'ro'
                          ? 'Pentru a adăuga preparate în coș sau a personaliza ingredientele, accesează secțiunea Meniu.'
                          : lang === 'it'
                          ? 'Per aggiungere piatti al carrello o personalizzare gli ingredienti, visita la sezione Menu.'
                          : 'To add items to your cart or customize ingredients, explore the Menu section.'}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <button
                    onClick={() => handleNavigateTo('menu')}
                    className="w-full sm:w-auto px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-[#241813] hover:bg-[#3D2C24] text-white text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold transition-all shadow-xs flex items-center justify-center gap-2 cursor-pointer shrink-0 active:scale-98"
                  >
                    <span>{lang === 'ro' ? 'Mergi la meniu' : lang === 'it' ? 'Vai al menu' : 'Go to menu'}</span>
                    <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
                  </button>
                </div>

                {/* CHEF LAB INVITATION BANNER */}
                <div className="relative overflow-hidden rounded-3xl bg-gradient-to-r from-[#241813] via-[#38261F] to-[#241813] p-6 sm:p-10 text-white border border-[#F0BE2C]/30 shadow-lg">
                  <div className="relative z-10 max-w-2xl">
                    <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1 rounded-full bg-[#F0BE2C]/20 border border-[#F0BE2C]/40 text-[#F0BE2C] text-xs font-bold mb-3">
                      <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                      <span>{lang === 'ro' ? 'Sandwich personalizat' : lang === 'it' ? 'Panino personalizzato' : 'Custom sandwich'}</span>
                    </div>
                    <h3 className="font-serif text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-bold mb-2">
                      {lang === 'ro'
                        ? 'Fii tu chef-ul! Intră în Chef Lab'
                        : lang === 'it'
                        ? 'Diventa tu lo chef! Entra nel Chef Lab'
                        : 'Be the chef! Step into Chef Lab'}
                    </h3>
                    <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-stone-300 leading-relaxed mb-6">
                      {lang === 'ro'
                        ? 'Alege schiacciata preferată, mezelurile autentice italiene, brânzeturile fine, sosurile gourmet și legumele proaspete. Vizualizează construcția strat cu strat!'
                        : lang === 'it'
                        ? 'Componi il tuo panino ideale strato dopo strato con ingredienti freschi preparati al momento.'
                        : 'Build your dream sandwich layer by layer with artisan Schiacciata, authentic Italian cuts, cheeses, sauces and veggies!'}
                    </p>
                    <button
                      id="home-open-cheflab-btn"
                      onClick={() => handleNavigateTo('builder')}
                      className="inline-flex items-center gap-2.5 px-6 py-3 rounded-full bg-[#F0BE2C] hover:bg-[#d8a71e] text-[#241813] font-bold text-sm shadow-md hover:shadow-lg transition-all cursor-pointer active:scale-95"
                    >
                      <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4 text-[#241813]" />
                      <span>
                        {lang === 'ro'
                          ? 'Deschide Chef Lab studio ✨'
                          : lang === 'it'
                          ? 'Apri Chef Lab studio ✨'
                          : 'Open Chef Lab studio ✨'}
                      </span>
                      <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </section>

            {/* Corporate & Event Catering with Instant Quote Simulator */}
            <CateringSection lang={lang} />

            {/* Location, Schedule & 1-Click GPS Navigation */}
            <LocationContactSection lang={lang} />
          </div>
        )}
      </main>

      {/* Comprehensive Footer */}
      <Footer
        onNavigateTo={handleNavigateTo}
        onOpenApiKeySettings={() => setIsApiKeyModalOpen(true)}
        lang={lang}
      />

      {/* MOBILE STICKY BOTTOM NAVIGATION BAR FOR PHONES */}
      <MobileBottomBar
        cartCount={totalCartCount}
        cartTotal={totalCartPrice}
        onOpenCart={() => setIsCartOpen(true)}
        onNavigateTo={handleNavigateTo}
        activeSection={currentPage}
        lang={lang}
      />

      {/* DESKTOP FLOATING BOTTOM CART BUTTON */}
      <button
        id="desktop-bottom-cart-btn"
        onClick={() => setIsCartOpen(true)}
        className="hidden md:flex fixed bottom-6 right-6 z-40 items-center gap-2.5 px-4 py-3 bg-[#C44E36] hover:bg-[#A63B25] text-white font-bold rounded-full shadow-lg hover:shadow-xl transition-all duration-200 cursor-pointer active:scale-95 border border-white/20"
        aria-label="Coș de cumpărături"
      >
        <div className="relative inline-flex">
          <ShoppingBag className="w-5 h-5" />
          <span
            id="desktop-cart-badge-dot"
            className={`absolute -top-1.5 -right-2.5 text-[10px] font-extrabold rounded-full min-w-[18px] h-[18px] px-1 flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs transition-colors ${
              totalCartCount > 0 ? 'bg-[#F0BE2C] text-[#241813]' : 'bg-white/30 text-white'
            }`}
          >
            {totalCartCount}
          </span>
        </div>
        <span className="text-sm font-bold">
          {lang === 'ro' ? 'Coș' : lang === 'it' ? 'Carrello' : 'Cart'}
          {totalCartPrice > 0 ? ` • ${totalCartPrice} lei` : ''}
        </span>
      </button>

      {/* Cart Drawer */}
      <CartDrawer
        isOpen={isCartOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsCartOpen(false)}
        cartItems={cartItems}
        onUpdateQuantity={handleUpdateQuantity}
        onRemoveItem={handleRemoveItem}
        onClearCart={handleClearCart}
        onExploreMenu={() => handleNavigateTo('menu')}
        lang={lang}
      />

      {/* Item Detail & Customization Modal */}
      <ItemDetailModal
        item={activeDetailItem}
        onClose={() => setActiveDetailItem(null)}
        onAddToCart={handleAddToCartFromModal}
        lang={lang}
      />

      {/* API Key & Cloud Settings Modal */}
      <ApiKeyModal
        isOpen={isApiKeyModalOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsApiKeyModalOpen(false)}
        lang={lang}
      />

      {/* Floating Toast Notification - Positioned above Mobile Bottom Bar */}
      {toastMessage && (
        <div
          id="app-toast-notification"
          className="fixed bottom-20 md:bottom-6 left-4 right-4 sm:left-auto sm:right-6 z-50 bg-[#241813] text-white px-4 py-3 rounded-2xl shadow-xl flex items-center gap-3 border border-[#F0BE2C]/40 animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-bottom-3 duration-200 max-w-sm sm:max-w-md mx-auto sm:mx-0"
        >
          <div className="w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-emerald-500 text-white flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
            <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          </div>
          <span className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold truncate">{toastMessage}</span>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
